Fully lifted blirp interpolation – a large deviation view
Mihailo Stojnic

Abstract




Abstract

[
104
]
introduced a powerful
fully lifted
(fl) statistical interpolating mechanism. It established a nested connection between blirps (bilinearly indexed random processes) and their decoupled (linearly indexed) comparative counterparts. We here revisit the comparison from
[
104
]
and introduce its a
large deviation
upgrade. The new machinery allows to substantially widen the
[
104
]
’s range of applicability. In addition to
typical
, studying analytically much harder
atypical
random structures features is now possible as well. To give a bit of a practical flavor, we show how the obtained results connect to the so-called
local entropies
(LE) and their predicated role in understanding solutions clustering and associated
computational gaps
in hard random optimization problems. As was the case in
[
104
]
, even though the technical considerations often appear as fairly involved, the final interpolating forms admit elegant expressions thereby providing a relatively easy to use tool readily available for further studies. Moreover, as the considered models encompass all well known random structures discussed in
[
104
]
, the obtained results automatically apply to them as well.

Index Terms: Random processes; comparisons; lifting; large deviations; local entropy
.

1
Introduction

In
[
104
]
a powerful
fully lifted
(fl) probabilistic blirp interpolating mechanism was introduced. It arrived as a strong upgrade on partially lifted concepts from
[
101
,
100
]
and the basic ones from
[
84
,
49
]
(see also, e.g.,
[
106
,
31
,
32
,
60
]
for early considerations as well as
[
101
,
5
,
67
,
64
,
107
]
for a brief history, relevance, and development overview). While the range of applicability in a variety of scientific fields is rather wide, applications in random optimizations are of our prevalent interest. They became particularly fruitful over the last two decades (some of the most prominent examples include, compressed sensing, machine learning, and neural network statistical studies; see, e.g.,
[
50
,
108
,
72
,
73
,
74
,
75
,
90
,
87
,
86
,
91
,
88
,
89
]
). Characterizing typical behavior of their various features ranging from standard optimization metrics (objective values, optimal solutions, relations between optimizing variables) to associated algorithmic ones (accuracy, speed, convergence) became possible in large part due to a strong progress made in understanding and developing powerful comparison mechanisms. For example, many of the above performance metrics often exhibit the so-called
phase-transition
(PT) phenomenon where they undergo an abrupt change as one moves from one region of system parameters to another. In that light,
[
90
,
87
,
86
,
88
,
91
]
present concrete comparative techniques that
precisely
demarcate the succeeding/failing parameters regions and the separating borderline between them – the so-called phase transition (PT) curve.

While sometimes successful, simple comparison forms often need a major upgrade as one faces intrinsically more complex structures. An easy to state (but notoriously difficult to solve) prototype example is the famous SK quadratic (or particularly polynomial/tensorial) model where classical Slepian/Gordon max comparison forms
[
84
,
49
,
106
,
31
,
32
,
60
]
come up short of enabling a precise characterization (see, e.g.,
[
50
,
108
,
72
,
73
,
74
,
75
]
). Similar intricacies are faced in a host of different models including quadratic, bilinear, or minmax forms appearing in a plethora of applications ranging from high-dimensional geometry and signal processing to machine learning and neural nets (see, e.g.,
[
96
,
97
,
94
,
93
,
98
,
99
]
and references therein). As observed in
[
50
,
108
,
72
,
73
,
74
,
75
]
for the quadratic/tensorial max forms, and in
[
101
,
100
,
104
,
102
]
for forms from
[
96
,
97
,
94
,
93
,
98
,
99
]
, the needed foundational upgrades are tightly connected to the core improvements in associated random processes’ comparisons.

1.1
An algorithmic hardness connection

The results of
[
50
,
108
,
72
,
73
,
74
,
75
,
101
,
100
,
104
,
102
]
relate to typical behavior most prominently represented via the so-called ground state regime. In recent years, a need for studying large deviations counterparts emerged as well. To a degree replacing the standard NP theory, the
computational gaps
became a golden standard concept in attempting to explain (in a practically more relevant way) mismatch between the theoretically best possible (the one unrestricted by time constraints) and the practically best achievable algorithmic performance (see, e.g.,
[
69
,
44
,
45
,
46
,
4
,
3
,
43
,
6
]
for examples of well known problems where such gaps are expected to exist). While resolving the computational gaps mysteries in general remains a grand challenge, a gigantic amount of extraordinary effort was put forth to at least demystify them in some concrete instances
[
44
,
45
,
46
,
13
,
14
,
18
,
15
,
12
,
16
,
2
,
22
,
23
,
41
]
. We single out two key features present in a majority od the existing approaches:
(i)
the Overlap gap property (OGP)
[
44
,
45
,
46
,
41
]
and
(ii)
the Local entropy (LE)
[
13
,
14
,
18
,
15
,
12
,
16
,
2
,
22
,
23
]
(other approachers have been developed as well
[
39
,
112
,
20
,
54
,
53
,
52
,
28
,
30
]
, but they are typically tailored towards more specific groups of algorithms such as low-degree functions/polynomials, sum of squares hierarchies, statistical queries, and so on). In addition to their potential role in understanding the mystery of computational gaps, they are of independent interest as they provide deep insights into a hierarchical/clustering organization of random optimization problems solutions. Both OGP and LE concepts are usually very difficult to analytically handle as they often rely on combinatorial or nonconvex geometric structures. Interestingly, a large deviation view of standard statistical mechanics approach to studying associated free energies (and their above mentioned ground states) allows for particularly elegant LEs formulations
[
14
,
13
]
. We here revisit the
fully lifted
(fl) interpolating comparison from
[
104
]
and introduce its a large deviation counterpart. Differently from
[
104
]
, which is intended for studying
typical
features of random structures, the large deviations nature of the machinery introduced here allows to substantially extend the range of applicability. In particular, studying analytically much harder
atypical
features is now possible as well. After we present the key technical details in Sections
2
-
4
, we in Section
5
reconnect back to LEs as atypical random optimizations features and recontextualize the role of the obtained comparison results within the realm of studying the associated computational gaps.

2
A large deviation bilinear comparison – first level of lifting

Consider sets
{\mathcal{X}}=\{{\bf x}^{(1)},{\bf x}^{(2)},\dots,{\bf x}^{(l)}\}
,
\bar{{\mathcal{X}}}=\{\bar{{\bf x}}^{(1)},\bar{{\bf x}}^{(2)},\dots,\bar{{\bf x}}^{(l)}\}
, with
{\bf x}^{(i)},\bar{{\bf x}}^{(i)}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{n}
and
{\mathcal{Y}}=\{{\bf y}^{(1)},{\bf y}^{(2)},\dots,{\bf y}^{(l)}\}
with
{\bf y}^{(i)}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{m}
. For vectors
{\bf p}=[{\bf p}_{0},{\bf p}_{1},{\bf p}_{2}]
and
{\bf q}=[{\bf q}_{0},{\bf q}_{1},{\bf q}_{2}]
with ordered components (
{\bf p}_{0}\geq{\bf p}_{1}\geq{\bf p}_{2}=0
and
{\bf q}_{0}\geq{\bf q}_{1}\geq{\bf q}_{2}=0
), real scalars
p,\beta>0
and
s
, and functions
f_{\bar{{\bf x}}^{(i)}}(\cdot):{\mathbb{R}}^{n}\rightarrow{\mathbb{R}}
, we are interested in the following

f(G,u^{(4,1)},u^{(4,2)},{\mathcal{X}},\bar{{\mathcal{X}}},{\mathcal{Y}},{\bf p},{\bf q},\beta,s,p,f_{\bar{{\bf x}}^{(i)}}(\cdot))=\frac{\log\left(\sum_{i_{3}=1}^{l}\left(\sum_{i_{1}=1}^{l}\left(\sum_{i_{2}=1}^{l}e^{\beta D_{00}^{(i_{1},i_{2},i_{3})}}\right)^{s}\right)^{p}\right)}{p|s|\sqrt{n}},

(1)

where

\displaystyle D_{00}^{(i_{1},i_{2},i_{3})}

\displaystyle\triangleq

\displaystyle\left(({\bf y}^{(i_{2})})^{T}G{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}+\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}(u^{(4,1)}+u^{(4,2)})+f_{\bar{{\bf x}}^{(i_{3})}}({\bf x}^{(i_{1})})\right).

(2)

Our focus is on random mediums and to that end we consider random matrices
G\in{\mathbb{R}}^{m\times n}
with i.i.d. standard normal components and random variables
u^{(4,1)}\sim{\mathcal{N}}(0,{\bf p}_{0}{\bf q}_{0}-{\bf p}_{1}{\bf q}_{1})
and
u^{(4,2)}\sim{\mathcal{N}}(0,{\bf p}_{1}{\bf q}_{1})
(all three,
u^{(4,1)}
,
u^{(4,2)}
, and
G
are independent of each other). Following into the footsteps of
[
104
]
, we take a scalar
{\bf m}=[{\bf m}_{1}]
and observe that the following function might be of critically importance for studying
f(G,u^{(4,1)},u^{(4,2)},{\mathcal{X}},\bar{{\mathcal{X}}},{\mathcal{Y}},{\bf p},{\bf q},\beta,s,p,f_{\bar{{\bf x}}^{(i)}}(\cdot))

\xi({\mathcal{X}},\bar{{\mathcal{X}}},{\mathcal{Y}},{\bf p},{\bf q},{\bf m},\beta,s,p,f_{\bar{{\bf x}}^{(i)}}(\cdot))\triangleq\frac{{\mathbb{E}}_{G,u^{(4,2)}}\log\left(\sum_{i_{3}=1}^{l}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{u^{(4,1)}}\left(\sum_{i_{1}=1}^{l}\left(\sum_{i_{2}=1}^{l}e^{\beta D_{00}^{(i_{1},i_{2},i_{3})}}\right)^{s}\right)^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p}\right)}{p|s|\sqrt{n}{\bf m}_{1}}.

(3)

We adopt the convention that
{\mathbb{E}}
without specified subscript denotes the expectation over any underlying randomness. On the other hand, if the subsciprt is specified then the expectation is only with respect to that specified randomness. Following the main ides of
[
101
,
100
,
104
]
, we study properties of
\xi({\mathcal{X}},\bar{{\mathcal{X}}},{\mathcal{Y}},{\bf p},{\bf q},{\bf m},\beta,s,p,f_{\bar{{\bf x}}^{(i)}}(\cdot))
via the following interpolating function
\psi(\cdot)

\psi(t)=\frac{{\mathbb{E}}_{G,u^{(4,2)},{\bf u}^{(2,2)},{\bf h}^{(2)}}\log\left(\sum_{i_{3}=1}^{l}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{u^{(4,1)},{\bf u}^{(2,1)},{\bf h}^{(1)}}\left(\sum_{i_{1}=1}^{l}\left(\sum_{i_{2}=1}^{l}e^{\beta D_{0}^{(i_{1},i_{2},i_{3})}}\right)^{s}\right)^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p}\right)}{p|s|\sqrt{n}{\bf m}_{1}},

(4)

where

\displaystyle D_{0}^{(i_{1},i_{2},i_{3})}

\displaystyle\triangleq

\displaystyle\sqrt{t}({\bf y}^{(i_{2})})^{T}G{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}+\sqrt{1-t}\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}({\bf y}^{(i_{2})})^{T}({\bf u}^{(2,1)}+{\bf u}^{(2,2)})

(5)

\displaystyle+\sqrt{t}\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}(u^{(4,1)}+u^{(4,2)})+\sqrt{1-t}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}({\bf h}^{(1)}+{\bf h}^{(2)})^{T}{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}+f_{\bar{{\bf x}}^{(i_{3})}}({\bf x}^{(i_{1})}).

The
m
dimensional vectors
{\bf u}^{(2,1)}
and
{\bf u}^{(2,2)}
are comprised of i.i.d. zero-mean Gaussians with variances
{\bf p}_{0}-{\bf p}_{1}
and
{\bf p}_{1}
, respectively. Analogously,
n
dimensional vectors
{\bf h}^{(1)}
and
{\bf h}^{(2)}
are comprised of i.i.d. zero-mean Gaussians with variances
{\bf q}_{0}-{\bf q}_{1}
and
{\bf q}_{1}
, respectively. Also,
{\bf u}^{(2,1)}
,
{\bf u}^{(2,2)}
,
{\bf h}^{(1)}
, and
{\bf h}^{(2)}
are independent among themselves and of
G
,
u^{(4,1)}
, and
u^{(4,2)}
as well.

From (
5
) one observes that
D_{0}^{(i_{1},i_{2},i_{3})}
is a Gaussian
bilinearly
indexed random process (blirp). Moreover, it is not that difficult to see that
\xi({\mathcal{X}},\bar{{\mathcal{X}}},{\mathcal{Y}},{\bf p},{\bf q},{\bf m},\beta,s,p,f_{\bar{{\bf x}}^{(i)}}(\cdot))=\psi(1)
and since
\psi(0)
is often easier to handle than
\psi(1)
, finding a way to connect
\psi(1)
and
\psi(0)
is rather desirable. Such a connection would then establish a connection between
\xi({\mathcal{X}},\bar{{\mathcal{X}}},{\mathcal{Y}},{\bf p},{\bf q},{\bf m},\beta,s,p,f_{\bar{{\bf x}}^{(i)}}(\cdot))
and
\psi(0)
and ultimately between the original,
bilinearly
indexed process and two
linearly
indexed decoupled alternatives.

To ensure the easiness of the exposition, we set

\displaystyle\bar{{\bf u}}^{(i_{1},1)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{G{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}}{\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}}

\displaystyle{\bf u}^{(i_{1},3,1)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{({\bf h}^{(1)})^{T}{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}}{\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}}

\displaystyle{\bf u}^{(i_{1},3,2)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{({\bf h}^{(2)})^{T}{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}}{\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}}.

(6)

Also, we denote by
\bar{{\bf u}}_{j}^{(i_{1},1)}
the
j
-th component of
\bar{{\bf u}}^{(i_{1},1)}
and write

\displaystyle\bar{{\bf u}}_{j}^{(i_{1},1)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{G_{j,1:n}{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}}{\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}},1\leq j\leq m,

(7)

where
G_{j,1:n}
is the
j
-th row of
G
. Clearly, for any fixed
i_{1}
,
\bar{{\bf u}}^{(i_{1},1)}
has i.i.d. standard normals elements. As stated above, the i.i.d. zero-mean Gaussians elements of
{\bf u}^{(2,1)}
and
{\bf u}^{(2,2)}
have respective variances
{\bf p}_{0}-{\bf p}_{1}
and
{\bf p}_{1}-{\bf p}_{2}
and the i.i.d. zero-mean Gaussians elements of
{\bf u}^{(i_{1},3,1)}
and
{\bf u}^{(i_{1},3,2)}
have respective variances
{\bf q}_{0}-{\bf q}_{1}
and
{\bf q}_{1}-{\bf q}_{2}
. After setting
{\mathcal{U}}_{r}=\{u^{(4,r)},{\bf u}^{(2,r)},{\bf h}^{(r)}\},r\in\{1,2\}
, (
4
) can be rewritten as

\psi(t)=\frac{{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\log\left(\sum_{i_{3}=1}^{l}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left(\sum_{i_{1}=1}^{l}\left(\sum_{i_{2}=1}^{l}A_{i_{3}}^{(i_{1},i_{2})}\right)^{s}\right)^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p}\right)}{p|s|\sqrt{n}{\bf m}_{1}}=\frac{{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\log\left(\sum_{i_{3}=1}^{l}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p}\right)}{p|s|\sqrt{n}{\bf m}_{1}},

(8)

where
\beta_{i_{1}}=\beta\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}
and

\displaystyle B^{(i_{1},i_{2})}

\displaystyle\triangleq

\displaystyle\sqrt{t}({\bf y}^{(i_{2})})^{T}\bar{{\bf u}}^{(i_{1},1)}+\sqrt{1-t}({\bf y}^{(i_{2})})^{T}({\bf u}^{(2,1)}+{\bf u}^{(2,2)})

\displaystyle D^{(i_{1},i_{2},i_{3})}

\displaystyle\triangleq

\displaystyle(B^{(i_{1},i_{2})}+\sqrt{t}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}(u^{(4,1)}+u^{(4,2)})+\sqrt{1-t}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}({\bf u}^{(i_{1},3,1)}+{\bf u}^{(i_{1},3,2)})+f_{\bar{{\bf x}}^{(i_{3})}}({\bf x}^{(i_{1})}))

\displaystyle A_{i_{3}}^{(i_{1},i_{2})}

\displaystyle\triangleq

\displaystyle e^{\beta_{i_{1}}D^{(i_{1},i_{2},i_{3})}}

\displaystyle C_{i_{3}}^{(i_{1})}

\displaystyle\triangleq

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{2}=1}^{l}A_{i_{3}}^{(i_{1},i_{2})}

\displaystyle Z_{i_{3}}

\displaystyle\triangleq

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}=1}^{l}\left(\sum_{i_{2}=1}^{l}A_{i_{3}}^{(i_{1},i_{2})}\right)^{s}=\sum_{i_{1}=1}^{l}(C_{i_{3}}^{(i_{1})})^{s}.

(9)

As
[
101
,
100
,
104
]
demonstrated, to establish a comparative connection between
\psi(1)
and
\psi(0)
, studying monotonicity of
\psi(t)
is particularly useful. The following derivative is therefore the key object of our focus

\displaystyle\frac{d\psi(t)}{dt}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{d}{dt}\left(\frac{{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\log\left(\sum_{i_{3}=1}^{l}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p}\right)}{p|s|\sqrt{n}{\bf m}_{1}}\right)

(10)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\sum_{i_{3}=1}^{l}\frac{\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p-1}}{|s|\sqrt{n}{\bf m}_{1}\left(\sum_{i_{3}=1}^{l}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p}\right)}\frac{d{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}}{dt}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\sum_{i_{3}=1}^{l}\frac{{\bf m}_{1}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p-1}}{|s|\sqrt{n}{\bf m}_{1}\left(\sum_{i_{3}=1}^{l}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p}\right)}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\frac{1}{Z_{i_{3}}^{1-{\bf m}_{1}}}\frac{dZ_{i_{3}}}{dt}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\sum_{i_{3}=1}^{l}\frac{{\bf m}_{1}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p-1}}{|s|\sqrt{n}{\bf m}_{1}\left(\sum_{i_{3}=1}^{l}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p}\right)}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\frac{1}{Z_{i_{3}}^{1-{\bf m}_{1}}}\frac{d\left(\sum_{i_{1}=1}^{l}\left(\sum_{i_{2}=1}^{l}A_{i_{3}}^{(i_{1},i_{2})}\right)^{s}\right)}{dt}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\sum_{i_{3}=1}^{l}\frac{s{\bf m}_{1}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p-1}}{|s|\sqrt{n}{\bf m}_{1}\left(\sum_{i_{3}=1}^{l}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p}\right)}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\frac{1}{Z_{i_{3}}^{1-{\bf m}_{1}}}\sum_{i=1}^{l}(C_{i_{3}}^{(i_{1})})^{s-1}

\displaystyle\times\sum_{i_{2}=1}^{l}\beta_{i_{1}}A_{i_{3}}^{(i_{1},i_{2})}\frac{dD^{(i_{1},i_{2},i_{3})}}{dt},

where

\displaystyle\frac{dD^{(i_{1},i_{2},i_{3})}}{dt}=\left(\frac{dB^{(i_{1},i_{2})}}{dt}+\frac{\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}(u^{(4,1)}+u^{(4,2)})}{2\sqrt{t}}-\frac{\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}({\bf u}^{(i_{1},3,1)}+{\bf u}^{(i_{1},3,2)})}{2\sqrt{1-t}}\right).

(11)

Recalling on (
2
) we also have

\displaystyle\frac{dB^{(i_{1},i_{2})}}{dt}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{d\left(\sqrt{t}({\bf y}^{(i_{2})})^{T}\bar{{\bf u}}^{(i_{1},1)}+\sqrt{1-t}({\bf y}^{(i_{2})})^{T}({\bf u}^{(2,1)}+{\bf u}^{(2,2)})\right)}{dt}

(12)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{j=1}^{m}\left(\frac{{\bf y}_{j}^{(i_{2})}\bar{{\bf u}}_{j}^{(i_{1},1)}}{2\sqrt{t}}-\frac{{\bf y}_{j}^{(i_{2})}{\bf u}_{j}^{(2,1)}}{2\sqrt{1-t}}-\frac{{\bf y}_{j}^{(i_{2})}{\bf u}_{j}^{(2,2)}}{2\sqrt{1-t}}\right).

Combining (
11
) and (
12
) we find

\displaystyle\frac{dD^{(i_{1},i_{2},i_{3})}}{dt}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{j=1}^{m}\left(\frac{{\bf y}_{j}^{(i_{2})}\bar{{\bf u}}_{j}^{(i_{1},1)}}{2\sqrt{t}}-\frac{{\bf y}_{j}^{(i_{2})}{\bf u}_{j}^{(2,1)}}{2\sqrt{1-t}}-\frac{{\bf y}_{j}^{(i_{2})}{\bf u}_{j}^{(2,2)}}{2\sqrt{1-t}}\right)

(13)

\displaystyle+\frac{\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}u^{(4,1)}}{2\sqrt{t}}+\frac{\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}u^{(4,2)}}{2\sqrt{t}}-\frac{\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}{\bf u}^{(i_{1},3,1)}}{2\sqrt{1-t}}-\frac{\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}{\bf u}^{(i_{1},3,2)}}{2\sqrt{1-t}}.

Following the strategy of
[
104
]
, we rearrange the above terms into three groups that depend on
G
,
{\mathcal{U}}_{2}
, and
{\mathcal{U}}_{1}
and write

\displaystyle\frac{dD^{(i_{1},i_{2},i_{3})}}{dt}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\bar{T}_{G}+\bar{T}_{2}+\bar{T}_{1},

(14)

where

\displaystyle\bar{T}_{G}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{j=1}^{m}\frac{{\bf y}_{j}^{(i_{2})}\bar{{\bf u}}_{j}^{(i_{1},1)}}{2\sqrt{t}}

\displaystyle\bar{T}_{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\sum_{j=1}^{m}\frac{{\bf y}_{j}^{(i_{2})}{\bf u}_{j}^{(2,2)}}{2\sqrt{1-t}}+\frac{\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}u^{(4,2)}}{2\sqrt{t}}-\frac{\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}{\bf u}^{(i_{1},3,2)}}{2\sqrt{1-t}}

\displaystyle\bar{T}_{1}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\sum_{j=1}^{m}\frac{{\bf y}_{j}^{(i_{2})}{\bf u}_{j}^{(2,1)}}{2\sqrt{1-t}}+\frac{\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}u^{(4,1)}}{2\sqrt{t}}-\frac{\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}{\bf u}^{(i_{1},3,1)}}{2\sqrt{1-t}}.

(15)

A combination of (
10
) and (
14
) gives

\frac{d\psi(t)}{dt}={\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\sum_{i_{3}=1}^{l}\frac{s{\bf m}_{1}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p-1}}{|s|\sqrt{n}{\bf m}_{1}\left(\sum_{i_{3}=1}^{l}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p}\right)}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\frac{1}{Z_{i_{3}}^{1-{\bf m}_{1}}}\sum_{i_{1}=1}^{l}(C_{i_{3}}^{(i_{1})})^{s-1}\sum_{i_{2}=1}^{l}\beta_{i_{1}}A_{i_{3}}^{(i_{1},i_{2})}\left(\bar{T}_{G}+\bar{T}_{2}+\bar{T}_{1}\right).

(16)

A bit of additional rearranging allows to write

\frac{d\psi(t)}{dt}=\frac{\mbox{sign}(s)}{2\sqrt{n}}\sum_{i_{1}=1}^{l}\sum_{i_{2}=1}^{l}\beta_{i_{1}}\left(T_{G}+T_{2}+T_{1}\right),

(17)

where

\displaystyle T_{G}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{j=1}^{m}\frac{T_{G,j}}{\sqrt{t}}

\displaystyle T_{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\sum_{j=1}^{m}\frac{T_{2,1,j}}{\sqrt{1-t}}-\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}\frac{T_{2,2}}{\sqrt{1-t}}+\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}\frac{T_{2,3}}{\sqrt{t}}

\displaystyle T_{1}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\sum_{j=1}^{m}\frac{T_{1,1,j}}{\sqrt{1-t}}-\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}\frac{T_{1,2}}{\sqrt{1-t}}+\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}\frac{T_{1,3}}{\sqrt{t}},

(18)

and

\displaystyle T_{G,j}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\left(\sum_{i_{3}=1}^{l}\frac{\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p-1}}{\left(\sum_{i_{3}=1}^{l}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p}\right)}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\frac{(C_{i_{3}}^{(i_{1})})^{s-1}A_{i_{3}}^{(i_{1},i_{2})}{\bf y}_{j}^{(i_{2})}\bar{{\bf u}}_{j}^{(i_{1},1)}}{Z_{i_{3}}^{1-{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)

\displaystyle T_{2,1,j}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\left(\sum_{i_{3}=1}^{l}\frac{\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p-1}}{\left(\sum_{i_{3}=1}^{l}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p}\right)}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\frac{(C_{i_{3}}^{(i_{1})})^{s-1}A_{i_{3}}^{(i_{1},i_{2})}{\bf y}_{j}^{(i_{2})}{\bf u}_{j}^{(2,2)}}{Z_{i_{3}}^{1-{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)

\displaystyle T_{2,2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\left(\sum_{i_{3}=1}^{l}\frac{\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p-1}}{\left(\sum_{i_{3}=1}^{l}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p}\right)}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\frac{(C_{i_{3}}^{(i_{1})})^{s-1}A_{i_{3}}^{(i_{1},i_{2})}{\bf u}^{(i_{1},3,2)}}{Z_{i_{3}}^{1-{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)

\displaystyle T_{2,3}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\left(\sum_{i_{3}=1}^{l}\frac{\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p-1}}{\left(\sum_{i_{3}=1}^{l}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p}\right)}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\frac{(C_{i_{3}}^{(i_{1})})^{s-1}A_{i_{3}}^{(i_{1},i_{2})}u^{(4,2)}}{Z_{i_{3}}^{1-{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)

\displaystyle T_{1,1,j}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\left(\sum_{i_{3}=1}^{l}\frac{\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p-1}}{\left(\sum_{i_{3}=1}^{l}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p}\right)}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\frac{(C_{i_{3}}^{(i_{1})})^{s-1}A_{i_{3}}^{(i_{1},i_{2})}{\bf y}_{j}^{(i_{2})}{\bf u}_{j}^{(2,1)}}{Z_{i_{3}}^{1-{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)

\displaystyle T_{1,2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\left(\sum_{i_{3}=1}^{l}\frac{\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p-1}}{\left(\sum_{i_{3}=1}^{l}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p}\right)}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\frac{(C_{i_{3}}^{(i_{1})})^{s-1}A_{i_{3}}^{(i_{1},i_{2})}{\bf u}^{(i_{1},3,1)}}{Z_{i_{3}}^{1-{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)

\displaystyle T_{1,3}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\left(\sum_{i_{3}=1}^{l}\frac{\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p-1}}{\left(\sum_{i_{3}=1}^{l}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p}\right)}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\frac{(C_{i_{3}}^{(i_{1})})^{s-1}A_{i_{3}}^{(i_{1},i_{2})}u^{(4,1)}}{Z_{i_{3}}^{1-{\bf m}_{1}}}\right),

(19)

where we recognize the above seven objects placed in the three mentioned groups as key components in the ensuing computations. Each of these terms will be handled separately. In the process of doing so, we heavily rely on
[
104
]
and follow the approach presented therein as closely as possible. However, we proceed in a much faster fashion. In particular, whenever possible, we avoid unnecessary repetitions of the already introduced concepts and instead focus on key differences.

2.1
Computing
\frac{d\psi(t)}{dt}

It turns out as convenient to choose a particular order in which we handle the above seven terms. We first handle the last group (
T_{1,1,j}
,
T_{1,2}
, and
T_{1,3}
) to which we refer as
T_{1}
–group, then we handle the middle group (
T_{2,1,j}
,
T_{2,2}
, and
T_{2,3}
) to which we refer as
T_{2}
–group, and at the end we handle
T_{G,j}
to which we refer as
T_{G}
–group.

2.1.1
T_{1}
–group – first level

Each of the three terms from
T_{1}
–group is handled separately.

Determining

T_{1,1,j}

Utilizing Gaussian integration by parts we have

\displaystyle T_{1,1,j}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\left(\sum_{i_{3}=1}^{l}\frac{\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p-1}}{\left(\sum_{i_{3}=1}^{l}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p}\right)}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\frac{(C_{i_{3}}^{(i_{1})})^{s-1}A_{i_{3}}^{(i_{1},i_{2})}{\bf y}_{j}^{(i_{2})}}{Z_{i_{3}}^{1-{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)

(20)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\left(\sum_{i_{3}=1}^{l}\frac{\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p-1}}{\left(\sum_{i_{3}=1}^{l}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p}\right)}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}({\bf u}_{j}^{(2,1)}{\bf u}_{j}^{(2,1)})\frac{d}{du_{j}^{(2,1)}}\left(\frac{(C_{i_{3}}^{(i_{1})})^{s-1}A_{i_{3}}^{(i_{1},i_{2})}{\bf y}_{j}^{(i_{2})}}{Z_{i_{3}}^{1-{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)\right)\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\left(\sum_{i_{3}=1}^{l}\frac{\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p-1}}{\left(\sum_{i_{3}=1}^{l}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p}\right)}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}({\bf u}_{j}^{(2,1)}{\bf u}_{j}^{(2,1)}){\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left(\frac{d}{du_{j}^{(2,1)}}\left(\frac{(C_{i_{3}}^{(i_{1})})^{s-1}A_{i_{3}}^{(i_{1},i_{2})}{\bf y}_{j}^{(i_{2})}}{Z_{i_{3}}^{1-{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)\right)\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle({\bf p}_{0}-{\bf p}_{1}){\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\left(\sum_{i_{3}=1}^{l}\frac{\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p-1}}{\left(\sum_{i_{3}=1}^{l}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p}\right)}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left(\frac{d}{du_{j}^{(2,1)}}\left(\frac{(C_{i_{3}}^{(i_{1})})^{s-1}A_{i_{3}}^{(i_{1},i_{2})}{\bf y}_{j}^{(i_{2})}}{Z_{i_{3}}^{1-{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)\right)\right).

Since the inner expectation on the right hand side of the last equality is structurally identical to the one in (19) in

]
, we can write the following analogue to
[

]
’s (20)

\displaystyle T_{1,1,j}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle({\bf p}_{0}-{\bf p}_{1}){\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\left(\sum_{i_{3}=1}^{l}\frac{\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p-1}}{\left(\sum_{i_{3}=1}^{l}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p}\right)}\left(\Theta_{1}+\Theta_{2}\right)\right),

with

\displaystyle\Theta_{1}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\Bigg(\Bigg.\frac{{\bf y}_{j}^{(i_{2})}\left((C_{i_{3}}^{(i_{1})})^{s-1}\beta_{i_{1}}A_{i_{3}}^{(i_{1},i_{2})}{\bf y}_{j}^{(i_{2})}\sqrt{1-t}+A_{i_{3}}^{(i_{1},i_{2})}(s-1)(C_{i_{3}}^{(i_{1})})^{s-2}\beta_{i_{1}}\sum_{p_{2}=1}^{l}A_{i_{3}}^{(i_{1},p_{2})}{\bf y}_{j}^{(p_{2})}\sqrt{1-t}\right)}{Z_{i_{3}}^{1-{\bf m}_{1}}}\Bigg.\Bigg)\Bigg.\Bigg)

\displaystyle\Theta_{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-(1-{\bf m}_{1}){\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left(\sum_{p_{1}=1}^{l}\frac{(C_{i_{3}}^{(i_{1})})^{s-1}A_{i_{3}}^{(i_{1},i_{2})}{\bf y}_{j}^{(i_{2})}}{Z_{i_{3}}^{2-{\bf m}_{1}}}s(C_{i_{3}}^{(p_{1})})^{s-1}\sum_{p_{2}=1}^{l}\beta_{p_{1}}A^{(p_{1},p_{2})}{\bf y}_{j}^{(p_{2})}\sqrt{1-t}\right)\Bigg.\Bigg).

Moreover, the following analogue to

104
]
’s (23) immediately follows as well

\displaystyle\hskip-21.68121pt\sum_{i_{1}=1}^{l}\sum_{i_{2}=1}^{l}\sum_{j=1}^{m}\left(\sum_{i_{3}=1}^{l}\frac{\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p-1}}{\left(\sum_{i_{3}=1}^{l}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p}\right)}\frac{\beta_{i_{1}}\Theta_{1}}{\sqrt{1-t}}\right)=

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\Bigg(\Bigg.\sum_{i_{3}=1}^{l}\frac{\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p}}{\left(\sum_{i_{3}=1}^{l}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p}\right)}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\frac{Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}}{{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}}\sum_{i_{1}=1}^{l}\frac{(C_{i_{3}}^{(i_{1})})^{s}}{Z_{i_{3}}}\sum_{i_{2}=1}^{l}\frac{A_{i_{3}}^{(i_{1},i_{2})}}{C_{i_{3}}^{(i_{1})}}\beta_{i_{1}}^{2}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}^{2}\Bigg)\Bigg.

\displaystyle\hskip-8.67204pt+\Bigg(\Bigg.\sum_{i_{3}=1}^{l}\frac{\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p}}{\left(\sum_{i_{3}=1}^{l}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p}\right)}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\frac{Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}}{{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}}\sum_{i_{1}=1}^{l}\frac{(s-1)(C_{i_{3}}^{(i_{1})})^{s}}{Z_{i_{3}}}\sum_{i_{2}=1}^{l}\sum_{p_{2}=1}^{l}\frac{A_{i_{3}}^{(i_{1},i_{2})}A_{i_{3}}^{(i_{1},p_{2})}}{(C_{i_{3}}^{(i_{1})})^{2}}\beta_{i_{1}}^{2}({\bf y}^{(p_{2})})^{T}{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\Bigg)\Bigg..

We introduce the operator

\displaystyle\Phi_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}^{(i_{3})}

\displaystyle\triangleq

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\frac{Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}}{{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}}\triangleq{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left(\frac{Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}}{{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}}\left(\cdot\right)\right),

and set

\displaystyle\gamma_{00}(i_{3})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p}}{\left(\sum_{i_{3}=1}^{l}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p}\right)}

\displaystyle\gamma_{0}(i_{1},i_{2};i_{3})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{(C_{i_{3}}^{(i_{1})})^{s}}{Z_{i_{3}}}\frac{A_{i_{3}}^{(i_{1},i_{2})}}{C_{i_{3}}^{(i_{1})}}

\displaystyle\gamma_{01}^{(1)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\gamma_{00}(i_{3})\Phi_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}^{(i_{3})}(\gamma_{0}(i_{1},i_{2};i_{3}))

\displaystyle\gamma_{02}^{(1)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\gamma_{00}(i_{3})\Phi_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}^{(i_{3})}(\gamma_{0}(i_{1},i_{2};i_{3})\times\gamma_{0}(i_{1},p_{2};i_{3}))

\displaystyle\gamma_{1}^{(1)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\gamma_{00}(i_{3})\Phi_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}^{(i_{3})}\left(\gamma_{0}(i_{1},i_{2};i_{3})\times\gamma_{0}(p_{1},p_{2};i_{3})\right)

\displaystyle.\gamma_{21}^{(1)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\gamma_{00}(i_{3})\Phi_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}^{(i_{3})}\gamma_{0}(i_{1},i_{2};i_{3})\times\gamma_{00}(p_{3})\Phi_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}^{(p_{3})}\gamma_{0}(p_{1},p_{2};p_{3})

\displaystyle\gamma_{22}^{(1)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\gamma_{00}(i_{3})\left(\Phi_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}^{(i_{3})}\gamma_{0}(i_{1},i_{2};i_{3})\times\Phi_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}^{(i_{3})}\gamma_{0}(p_{1},p_{2};i_{3})\right).

(25)

It is relatively easy to check that all the above
\gamma
’s are valid measures. For example, we have

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{3}=1}^{l}\sum_{i_{1}=1}^{l}\sum_{i_{2}=1}^{l}\gamma_{01}^{(1)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{3}=1}^{l}\sum_{i_{1}=1}^{l}\sum_{i_{2}=1}^{l}\gamma_{00}(i_{3})\Phi_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}^{(i_{3})}(\gamma_{0}(i_{1},i_{2};i_{3}))

(26)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{3}=1}^{l}\sum_{i_{1}=1}^{l}\sum_{i_{2}=1}^{l}\gamma_{00}(i_{3}){\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\frac{Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}}{{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}}\frac{(C_{i_{3}}^{(i_{1})})^{s}}{Z_{i_{3}}}\frac{A_{i_{3}}^{(i_{1},i_{2})}}{C_{i_{3}}^{(i_{1})}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{3}=1}^{l}\gamma_{00}(i_{3}){\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\frac{Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}}{{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}}\sum_{i_{1}=1}^{l}\frac{(C_{i_{3}}^{(i_{1})})^{s}}{Z_{i_{3}}}\sum_{i_{2}=1}^{l}\frac{A_{i_{3}}^{(i_{1},i_{2})}}{C_{i_{3}}^{(i_{1})}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{3}=1}^{l}\gamma_{00}(i_{3}){\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\frac{Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}}{{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{3}=1}^{l}\gamma_{00}(i_{3})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 1,

where the fourth equality follows by the definitions of
Z_{i_{3}}
and
C_{i_{3}}^{(i_{1})}
from (
2
). Keeping in mind that one trivially has
\gamma_{01}^{(1)}\geq 0
, (
26
) ensures that
\gamma_{01}^{(1)}
is indeed a measure. The proofs for other
\gamma
’s are identical and we skip them. As a convention,
\langle\cdot\rangle_{a}
will denote the average with respect to measure
a
. Additionally, since each
\gamma
measure (and
\Phi(\cdot)
operator) defined above are also functions of

, all functions that depend on them will be functions of
t
as well. We skip stating this explicitly to lighten the notation and facilitate writing.

Utilizing (
2.1.1
) we can rewrite (
2.1.1
) as

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}=1}^{l}\sum_{i_{2}=1}^{l}\sum_{j=1}^{m}\left(\sum_{i_{3}=1}^{l}\frac{\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p-1}}{\left(\sum_{i_{3}=1}^{l}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p}\right)}\frac{\beta_{i_{1}}\Theta_{1}}{\sqrt{1-t}}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\beta^{2}\Bigg(\Bigg.\langle\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}^{2}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}^{2}\rangle_{\gamma_{01}^{(1)}}

(27)

\displaystyle+(s-1)\langle\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}^{2}({\bf y}^{(p_{2})})^{T}{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\rangle_{\gamma_{02}^{(1)}}\Bigg)\Bigg..

In an analogous fashion, from (
2.1.1
) we find

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}=1}^{l}\sum_{i_{2}=1}^{l}\sum_{j=1}^{m}\left(\sum_{i_{3}=1}^{l}\frac{\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p-1}}{\left(\sum_{i_{3}=1}^{l}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p}\right)}\frac{\beta_{i_{1}}\Theta_{2}}{\sqrt{1-t}}\right)\hskip-5.05942pt

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\hskip-3.61371pt-s(1-{\bf m}_{1}){\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\Bigg(\Bigg.\sum_{i_{3}=1}^{l}\frac{\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p}}{\left(\sum_{i_{3}=1}^{l}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p}\right)}

\displaystyle\hskip-3.61371pt\times\frac{Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}}{{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}}\sum_{i_{1}=1}^{l}\frac{(C_{i_{3}}^{(i_{1})})^{s}}{Z_{i_{3}}}\sum_{i_{2}=1}^{l}\frac{A_{i_{3}}^{(i_{1},i_{2})}}{C_{i_{3}}^{(i_{1})}}

\displaystyle\hskip-3.61371pt\times\sum_{p_{1}=1}^{l}\frac{(C_{i_{3}}^{(p_{1})})^{s}}{Z_{i_{3}}}\sum_{p_{2}=1}^{l}\frac{A_{i_{3}}^{(p_{1},p_{2})}}{C_{i_{3}}^{(p_{1})}}\beta_{i_{1}}\beta_{p_{1}}({\bf y}^{(p_{2})})^{T}{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\Bigg.\Bigg)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\hskip-3.61371pt-s\beta^{2}(1-{\bf m}_{1}){\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\langle\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}\|{\bf x}^{(p_{1})}\|_{2}({\bf y}^{(p_{2})})^{T}{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\rangle_{\gamma_{1}^{(1)}}.

A combination of (

\displaystyle-({\bf p}_{0}-{\bf p}_{1})s\beta^{2}(1-{\bf m}_{1})\langle\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}\|{\bf x}^{(p_{1})}\|_{2}({\bf y}^{(p_{2})})^{T}{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\rangle_{\gamma_{1}^{(1)}}.

Determining

T_{1,2}

Following further the Gaussian integration by parts path we have

\displaystyle T_{1,2}\hskip 0.0pt

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\left(\sum_{i_{3}=1}^{l}\frac{\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p-1}}{\left(\sum_{i_{3}=1}^{l}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p}\right)}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\frac{(C_{i_{3}}^{(i_{1})})^{s-1}A_{i_{3}}^{(i_{1},i_{2})}{\bf u}^{(i_{1},3,1)}}{Z_{i_{3}}^{1-{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\left(\sum_{i_{3}=1}^{l}\frac{\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p-1}}{\left(\sum_{i_{3}=1}^{l}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p}\right)}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\sum_{p_{1}=1}^{l}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}({\bf u}^{(i_{1},3,1)}{\bf u}^{(p_{1},3,1)})\frac{d}{d{\bf u}^{(p_{1},3,1)}}\left(\frac{(C_{i_{3}}^{(i_{1})})^{s-1}A_{i_{3}}^{(i_{1},i_{2})}}{Z_{i_{3}}^{1-{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\left(\sum_{i_{3}=1}^{l}\frac{({\bf q}_{0}-{\bf q}_{1})\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p-1}}{\left(\sum_{i_{3}=1}^{l}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p}\right)}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\sum_{p_{1}=1}^{l}\frac{({\bf x}^{(i_{1})})^{T}{\bf x}^{(p_{1})}}{\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}\|{\bf x}^{(p_{1})}\|_{2}}\frac{d}{d{\bf u}^{(p_{1},3,1)}}\left(\frac{(C_{i_{3}}^{(i_{1})})^{s-1}A_{i_{3}}^{(i_{1},i_{2})}}{Z_{i_{3}}^{1-{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)\right).

Since the inner expectation on the most right hand side of the last equality is structurally identical to the corresponding one in
[
104
]
’s (30), one can immediately write the following analogue to
[

]
’s (31)

\displaystyle T_{1,2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\left(\sum_{i_{3}=1}^{l}\frac{({\bf q}_{0}-{\bf q}_{1})\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p-1}}{\left(\sum_{i_{3}=1}^{l}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p}\right)}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\sum_{p_{1}=1}^{l}\frac{({\bf x}^{(i_{1})})^{T}{\bf x}^{(p_{1})}}{\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}\|{\bf x}^{(p_{1})}\|_{2}}\frac{d}{d{\bf u}^{(p_{1},3,1)}}\left(\frac{(C_{i_{3}}^{(i_{1})})^{s-1}A_{i_{3}}^{(i_{1},i_{2})}}{Z_{i_{3}}^{1-{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)\right)

(31)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\Bigg(\Bigg.\sum_{i_{3}=1}^{l}\frac{({\bf q}_{0}-{\bf q}_{1})\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p-1}}{\left(\sum_{i_{3}=1}^{l}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p}\right)}

\displaystyle\times{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left(\frac{(C_{i_{3}}^{(i_{1})})^{s-1}\beta_{i_{1}}A_{i_{3}}^{(i_{1},i_{2})}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}\sqrt{1-t}+A_{i_{3}}^{(i_{1},i_{2})}(s-1)(C_{i_{3}}^{(i_{1})})^{s-2}\beta_{i_{1}}\sum_{p_{2}=1}^{l}A_{i_{3}}^{(i_{1},p_{2})}\|{\bf y}^{(p_{2})}\|_{2}\sqrt{1-t}}{Z_{i_{3}}^{1-{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)\Bigg.\Bigg)

\displaystyle-{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\Bigg(\Bigg.\sum_{i_{3}=1}^{l}\frac{({\bf q}_{0}-{\bf q}_{1})(1-{\bf m}_{1})\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p-1}}{\left(\sum_{i_{3}=1}^{l}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p}\right)}

\displaystyle\times{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left(\sum_{p_{1}=1}^{l}\frac{({\bf x}^{(i_{1})})^{T}{\bf x}^{(p_{1})}}{\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}\|{\bf x}^{(p_{1})}\|_{2}}\frac{(C_{i_{3}}^{(i_{1})})^{s-1}A_{i_{3}}^{(i_{1},i_{2})}}{Z_{i_{3}}^{2-{\bf m}_{1}}}s(C_{i_{3}}^{(p_{1})})^{s-1}\sum_{p_{2}=1}^{l}\beta_{p_{1}}A_{i_{3}}^{(p_{1},p_{2})}\|{\bf y}^{(p_{2})}\|_{2}\sqrt{1-t}\right)\Bigg.\Bigg).

Utilizing (
2.1.1
) we then also have

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}=1}^{l}\sum_{i_{2}=1}^{l}\beta_{i_{1}}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}\frac{T_{1,2}}{\sqrt{1-t}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle({\bf q}_{0}-{\bf q}_{1})\beta^{2}

(32)

\displaystyle\times\left({\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\langle\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}^{2}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}^{2}\rangle_{\gamma_{01}^{(1)}}+(s-1){\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\langle\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}^{2}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}\|{\bf y}^{(p_{2})}\|_{2}\rangle_{\gamma_{02}^{(1)}}\right)

\displaystyle-({\bf q}_{0}-{\bf q}_{1})s\beta^{2}(1-{\bf m}_{1}){\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\langle({\bf x}^{(p_{1})})^{T}{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}\|{\bf y}^{(p_{2})}\|_{2}\rangle_{\gamma_{1}^{(1)}}.

Determining

T_{1,3}

Proceeding again via Gaussian integration by parts we find

\displaystyle T_{1,3}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\left(\sum_{i_{3}=1}^{l}\frac{\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p-1}}{\left(\sum_{i_{3}=1}^{l}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p}\right)}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\frac{(C^{(i_{1})})^{s-1}A^{(i_{1},i_{2})}u^{(4,1)}}{Z^{1-{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)

(33)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\left(\sum_{i_{3}=1}^{l}\frac{\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p-1}}{\left(\sum_{i_{3}=1}^{l}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p}\right)}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}(u^{(4,1)}u^{(4,1)})\left(\frac{d}{du^{(4,1)}}\left(\frac{(C^{(i_{1})})^{s-1}A^{(i_{1},i_{2})}u^{(4,1)}}{Z^{1-{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)\right)\right)\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle({\bf p}_{0}{\bf q}_{0}-{\bf p}_{1}{\bf q}_{1}){\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\left(\sum_{i_{3}=1}^{l}\frac{\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p-1}}{\left(\sum_{i_{3}=1}^{l}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p}\right)}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left(\frac{d}{du^{(4,1)}}\left(\frac{(C^{(i_{1})})^{s-1}A^{(i_{1},i_{2})}u^{(4,1)}}{Z^{1-{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)\right)\right).

Recognizing that the last inner expectation is structurally identical to the corresponding one in
[
104
]
’s (33) allows us to immediately write the following analogues to
[
104
]
’s (34) and (35)

\displaystyle T_{1,3}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\left(\sum_{i_{3}=1}^{l}\frac{({\bf p}_{0}{\bf q}_{0}-{\bf p}_{1}{\bf q}_{1})\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p-1}}{\left(\sum_{i_{3}=1}^{l}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p}\right)}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left(\frac{d}{du^{(4,1)}}\left(\frac{(C_{i_{3}}^{(i_{1})})^{s-1}A_{i_{3}}^{(i_{1},i_{2})}u^{(4,1)}}{Z_{i_{3}}^{1-{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)\right)\right)

(34)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\Bigg(\Bigg.\sum_{i_{3}=1}^{l}\frac{({\bf p}_{0}{\bf q}_{0}-{\bf p}_{1}{\bf q}_{1})\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p-1}}{\left(\sum_{i_{3}=1}^{l}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p}\right)}

\displaystyle\times{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left(\frac{(C_{i_{3}}^{(i_{1})})^{s-1}\beta_{i_{1}}A_{i_{3}}^{(i_{1},i_{2})}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}\sqrt{t}+A_{i_{3}}^{(i_{1},i_{2})}(s-1)(C_{i_{3}}^{(i_{1})})^{s-2}\beta_{i_{1}}\sum_{p_{2}=1}^{l}A_{i_{3}}^{(i_{1},p_{2})}\|{\bf y}^{(p_{2})}\|_{2}\sqrt{t}}{Z_{i_{3}}^{1-{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)\Bigg.\Bigg)

\displaystyle-{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\Bigg(\Bigg.\sum_{i_{3}=1}^{l}\frac{({\bf p}_{0}{\bf q}_{0}-{\bf p}_{1}{\bf q}_{1})\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p-1}}{\left(\sum_{i_{3}=1}^{l}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p}\right)}

\displaystyle\times{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left(\frac{(C_{i_{3}}^{(i_{1})})^{s-1}A_{i_{3}}^{(i_{1},i_{2})}}{Z_{i_{3}}^{2-{\bf m}_{1}}}s\sum_{p_{1}=1}^{l}(C_{i_{3}}^{(p_{1})})^{s-1}\sum_{p_{2}=1}^{l}\beta_{p_{1}}A_{i_{3}}^{(p_{1},p_{2})}\|{\bf y}^{(p_{2})}\|_{2}\sqrt{t}\right)\Bigg.\Bigg),

and

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}=1}^{l}\sum_{i_{2}=1}^{l}\beta_{i_{1}}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}\frac{T_{1,3}}{\sqrt{t}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle({\bf p}_{0}{\bf q}_{0}-{\bf p}_{1}{\bf q}_{1})\beta^{2}

\displaystyle\times\left({\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\langle\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}^{2}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}^{2}\rangle_{\gamma_{01}^{(1)}}+(s-1){\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\langle\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}^{2}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}\|{\bf y}^{(p_{2})}\|_{2}\rangle_{\gamma_{02}^{(1)}}\right)

\displaystyle-({\bf p}_{0}{\bf q}_{0}-{\bf p}_{1}{\bf q}_{1})s\beta^{2}(1-{\bf m}_{1}){\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\langle\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}\|{\bf x}^{(p_{`})}\|_{2}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}\|{\bf y}^{(p_{2})}\|_{2}\rangle_{\gamma_{1}^{(1)}}.

2.1.2
T_{2}
–group – first level

Following the practice established in the previous subsection, each of the three terms from
T_{2}
-group are handled separately.

Determining

T_{2,1,j}

After applying the Gaussian integration by parts we have

\displaystyle T_{2,1,j}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\left(\sum_{i_{3}=1}^{l}\frac{\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p-1}}{\left(\sum_{i_{3}=1}^{l}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p}\right)}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\frac{(C_{i_{3}}^{(i_{1})})^{s-1}A_{i_{3}}^{(i_{1},i_{2})}{\bf y}_{j}^{(i_{2})}{\bf u}_{j}^{(2,2)}}{Z_{i_{3}}^{1-{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{2},{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\sum_{i_{3}=1}^{l}\frac{(C_{i_{3}}^{(i_{1})})^{s-1}A_{i_{3}}^{(i_{1},i_{2})}{\bf y}_{j}^{(i_{2})}{\bf u}_{j}^{(2,2)}}{Z_{i_{3}}^{1-{\bf m}_{1}}\left(\sum_{i_{3}=1}^{l}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p}\right)}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p-1}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left(\sum_{i_{3}=1}^{l}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}({\bf u}_{j}^{(2,2)}{\bf u}_{j}^{(2,2)})\frac{d}{d{\bf u}_{j}^{(2,2)}}\left(\frac{(C_{i_{3}}^{(i_{1})})^{s-1}A_{i_{3}}^{(i_{1},i_{2})}{\bf y}_{j}^{(i_{2})}}{Z_{i_{3}}^{1-{\bf m}_{1}}\left(\sum_{i_{3}=1}^{l}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p}\right)}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p-1}\right)\right)\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{2},{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left(\sum_{i_{3}=1}^{l}\frac{{\bf p}_{1}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p-1}}{\left(\sum_{i_{3}=1}^{l}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p}\right)}\frac{d}{d{\bf u}_{j}^{(2,2)}}\left(\frac{(C_{i_{3}}^{(i_{1})})^{s-1}A_{i_{3}}^{(i_{1},i_{2})}{\bf y}_{j}^{(i_{2})}}{Z_{i_{3}}^{1-{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)\right)

\displaystyle+{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{2},{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left(\sum_{i_{3}=1}^{l}\frac{{\bf p}_{1}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p-1}\left((C_{i_{3}}^{(i_{1})})^{s-1}A_{i_{3}}^{(i_{1},i_{2})}{\bf y}_{j}^{(i_{2})}\right)}{Z_{i_{3}}^{1-{\bf m}_{1}}}\frac{d}{d{\bf u}_{j}^{(2,2)}}\left(\frac{1}{\left(\sum_{i_{3}=1}^{l}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p}\right)}\right)\right)

\displaystyle+{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{2},{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left(\sum_{i_{3}=1}^{l}\frac{{\bf p}_{1}\left((C_{i_{3}}^{(i_{1})})^{s-1}A_{i_{3}}^{(i_{1},i_{2})}{\bf y}_{j}^{(i_{2})}\right)}{Z_{i_{3}}^{1-{\bf m}_{1}}\left(\sum_{i_{3}=1}^{l}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p}\right)}\frac{d}{d{\bf u}_{j}^{(2,2)}}\left(\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p-1}\right)\right).

To make writing a bit more convenient we set

\displaystyle T_{2,1,j}=T_{2,1,j}^{c}+T_{2,1,j}^{d}+T_{2,1,j}^{e},

(37)

where

\displaystyle T_{2,1,j}^{c}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle T_{2,1,j}^{d}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{2},{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left(\sum_{i_{3}=1}^{l}\frac{{\bf p}_{1}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p-1}\left((C_{i_{3}}^{(i_{1})})^{s-1}A_{i_{3}}^{(i_{1},i_{2})}{\bf y}_{j}^{(i_{2})}\right)}{Z_{i_{3}}^{1-{\bf m}_{1}}}\frac{d}{d{\bf u}_{j}^{(2,2)}}\left(\frac{1}{\left(\sum_{i_{3}=1}^{l}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p}\right)}\right)\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{2},{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left(\sum_{i_{3}=1}^{l}\frac{{\bf p}_{1}\left((C_{i_{3}}^{(i_{1})})^{s-1}A_{i_{3}}^{(i_{1},i_{2})}{\bf y}_{j}^{(i_{2})}\right)}{Z_{i_{3}}^{1-{\bf m}_{1}}\left(\sum_{i_{3}=1}^{l}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p}\right)}\frac{d}{d{\bf u}_{j}^{(2,2)}}\left(\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p-1}\right)\right).

Moreover, we observe that scaling

by
{\bf p}_{1}
gives the term structurally identical to the one considered in the first part of Section

scaled by
({\bf p}_{0}-{\bf p}_{1})
. That further allows to write

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}=1}^{l}\sum_{i_{2}=1}^{l}\sum_{j=1}^{m}\beta_{i_{1}}\frac{T_{2,1,j}^{c}}{\sqrt{1-t}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-{\bf p}_{1}s\beta^{2}(1-{\bf m}_{1}){\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\langle\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}\|{\bf x}^{(p_{1})}\|_{2}({\bf y}^{(p_{2})})^{T}{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\rangle_{\gamma_{1}^{(1)}}.

To determine
T_{2,1,j}^{d}
we start with

\displaystyle\frac{d}{d{\bf u}_{j}^{(2,2)}}\left(\frac{1}{\left(\sum_{i_{3}=1}^{l}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p}\right)}\right)=-p\sum_{p_{3}=1}^{l}\frac{\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{p_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p-1}}{\left(\sum_{i_{3}=1}^{l}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p}\right)^{2}}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\frac{dZ_{p_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}}{d{\bf u}_{j}^{(2,2)}}.

From

]
’s (41) we have

) and (

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-p\sum_{p_{3}=1}^{l}\frac{\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{p_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p-1}}{\left(\sum_{i_{3}=1}^{l}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p}\right)^{2}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-s\sqrt{1-t}{\bf p}_{1}{\bf m}_{1}p{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{2},{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\Bigg(\Bigg.\sum_{i_{3}=1}^{l}\frac{\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p-1}\left((C_{i_{3}}^{(i_{1})})^{s-1}A_{i_{3}}^{(i_{1},i_{2})}{\bf y}_{j}^{(i_{2})}\right)}{Z_{i_{3}}^{1-{\bf m}_{1}}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-s\sqrt{1-t}{\bf p}_{1}{\bf m}_{1}p{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\Bigg(\Bigg.\sum_{i_{3}=1}^{l}\frac{\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p}}{\left(\sum_{i_{3}=1}^{l}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p}\right)}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\frac{Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}}{{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}}\frac{(C_{i_{3}}^{(i_{1})})^{s}}{Z_{i_{3}}}\frac{A_{i_{3}}^{(i_{1},i_{2})}}{C_{i_{3}}^{(i_{1})}}{\bf y}_{j}^{(i_{2})}

\displaystyle\times\left(\sum_{p_{3}=1}^{l}\frac{\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{p_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p}}{\left(\sum_{i_{3}=1}^{l}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p}\right)}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\frac{Z_{p_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}}{{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{p_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}}\sum_{p_{1}=1}^{l}\frac{(C_{p_{3}}^{(p_{1})})^{s}}{Z_{p_{3}}}\sum_{p_{2}=1}^{l}\frac{A_{p_{3}}^{(p_{1},p_{2})}}{C_{p_{3}}^{(p,1)}}\beta_{p_{1}}{\bf y}_{j}^{(p_{2})}\right)\Bigg.\Bigg).

It will turn out as convenient to also note the following

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}=1}^{l}\sum_{i_{2}=1}^{l}\sum_{j=1}^{m}\beta_{i_{1}}\frac{T_{2,1,j}^{d}}{\sqrt{1-t}}

\displaystyle\times{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\frac{Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}}{{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}}\sum_{i_{1}=1}^{l}\frac{(C_{i_{3}}^{(i_{1})})^{s}}{Z_{i_{3}}}\sum_{i_{2}=1}^{l}\frac{A_{i_{3}}^{(i_{1},i_{2})}}{C_{i_{3}}^{(i_{1})}}\beta_{i_{1}}{\bf y}_{j}^{(i_{2})}

\displaystyle-s{\bf p}_{1}{\bf m}_{1}p{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\langle\beta_{p_{1}}\beta_{i_{1}}({\bf y}^{(p_{2})})^{T}{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\rangle_{\gamma_{21}^{(1)}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-s\beta^{2}{\bf p}_{1}{\bf m}_{1}p{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\langle\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}\|{\bf x}^{(p_{1})}\|_{2}({\bf y}^{(p_{2})})^{T}{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\rangle_{\gamma_{21}^{(1)}}.

) in (
2.1.2

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{2},{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\Bigg(\Bigg.\sum_{i_{3}=1}^{l}\frac{{\bf p}_{1}\left((C_{i_{3}}^{(i_{1})})^{s-1}A_{i_{3}}^{(i_{1},i_{2})}{\bf y}_{j}^{(i_{2})}\right)}{Z_{i_{3}}^{1-{\bf m}_{1}}\left(\sum_{i_{3}=1}^{l}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p}\right)}

(47)

\displaystyle\times(p-1)\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p-2}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\frac{{\bf m}_{1}}{Z_{i_{3}}^{1-{\bf m}_{1}}}s\sum_{p_{1}=1}^{l}(C_{i_{3}}^{(p_{1})})^{s-1}\sum_{p_{2}=1}^{l}\beta_{p_{1}}A_{i_{3}}^{(p_{1},p_{2})}{\bf y}_{j}^{(p_{2})}\sqrt{1-t}\Bigg.\Bigg)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\bf p}_{1}s{\bf m}_{1}(p-1){\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\Bigg(\Bigg.\sum_{i_{3}=1}^{l}\frac{\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p}}{\left(\sum_{i_{3}=1}^{l}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p}\right)}

\displaystyle\times{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\frac{Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}}{{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}}\frac{\left((C_{i_{3}}^{(i_{1})})^{s}A_{i_{3}}^{(i_{1},i_{2})}{\bf y}_{j}^{(i_{2})}\right)}{Z_{i_{3}}C_{i_{3}}^{(i_{1})}}

\displaystyle\times{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\frac{Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}}{{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}}\sum_{p_{1}=1}^{l}\frac{(C_{i_{3}}^{(p_{1})})^{s}}{Z_{i_{3}}}\sum_{p_{2}=1}^{l}\frac{\beta_{p_{1}}A_{i_{3}}^{(p_{1},p_{2})}{\bf y}_{j}^{(p_{2})}\sqrt{1-t}}{C_{i_{3}}^{(p_{1})}}\Bigg.\Bigg),

and then

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}=1}^{l}\sum_{i_{2}=1}^{l}\beta_{i_{1}}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}\frac{T_{2,2}^{e}}{\sqrt{1-t}}

\displaystyle s\beta^{2}{\bf p}_{1}{\bf m}_{1}(p-1){\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\langle\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}\|{\bf x}^{(p_{1})}\|_{2}({\bf y}^{(p_{2})})^{T}{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\rangle_{\gamma_{22}^{(1)}}.

Combining (

), (

) we obtain

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}=1}^{l}\sum_{i_{2}=1}^{l}\sum_{j=1}^{m}\beta_{i_{1}}\frac{T_{2,1,j}}{\sqrt{1-t}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-s\beta^{2}{\bf p}_{1}{\bf m}_{1}{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\langle\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}\|{\bf x}^{(p_{1})}\|_{2}({\bf y}^{(p_{2})})^{T}{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\rangle_{\gamma_{21}^{(1)}}

\displaystyle+s\beta^{2}{\bf p}_{1}{\bf m}_{1}(p-1){\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\langle\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}\|{\bf x}^{(p_{1})}\|_{2}({\bf y}^{(p_{2})})^{T}{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\rangle_{\gamma_{22}^{(1)}}.

Determining

T_{2,2}

As earlier, we apply the Gaussian integration by parts to find

\displaystyle T_{2,2}\hskip-3.61371pt

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\left(\sum_{i_{3}=1}^{l}\frac{\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p-1}}{\left(\sum_{i_{3}=1}^{l}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p}\right)}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\frac{(C_{i_{3}}^{(i_{1})})^{s-1}A_{i_{3}}^{(i_{1},i_{2})}{\bf u}^{(i_{1},3,2)}}{Z_{i_{3}}^{1-{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{2},{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left(\sum_{i_{3}=1}^{l}\frac{\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p-1}}{\left(\sum_{i_{3}=1}^{l}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p}\right)}\frac{(C_{i_{3}}^{(i_{1})})^{s-1}A_{i_{3}}^{(i_{1},i_{2})}{\bf u}^{(i_{1},3,2)}}{Z_{i_{3}}^{1-{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)

\displaystyle+{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{2},{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\Bigg(\Bigg.\sum_{i_{3}=1}^{l}\frac{{\bf q}_{1}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p-1}(C_{i_{3}}^{(i_{1})})^{s-1}A_{i_{3}}^{(i_{1},i_{2})}}{Z_{i_{3}}^{1-{\bf m}_{1}}}

where we recall on

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}({\bf u}^{(i_{1},3,2)}{\bf u}^{(p_{1},3,2)})

\displaystyle T_{2,3}^{e}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\bf p}_{1}{\bf q}_{1}{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{2},{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left(\sum_{i_{3}=1}^{l}\frac{(C_{i_{3}}^{(i_{1})})^{s-1}A_{i_{3}}^{(i_{1},i_{2})}}{\left(\sum_{i_{3}=1}^{l}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p}\right)Z_{i_{3}}^{1-{\bf m}_{1}}}\left(\frac{d}{du^{(4,2)}}\left(\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p-1}\right)\right)\right).

(67)

After recognizing that
\frac{T_{2,3}^{c}}{{\bf p}_{1}{\bf q}_{1}}=\frac{T_{1,3}}{{\bf p}_{0}{\bf q}_{0}-{\bf p}_{1}{\bf q}_{1}}
, one, based on (

\displaystyle{\bf p}_{1}{\bf q}_{1}\beta^{2}\Bigg(\Bigg.{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\langle\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}^{2}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}^{2}\rangle_{\gamma_{01}^{(1)}}

(68)

\displaystyle\times+(s-1){\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\langle\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}^{2}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}\|{\bf y}^{(p_{2})}\|_{2}\rangle_{\gamma_{02}^{(1)}}\Bigg.\Bigg)

\displaystyle-{\bf p}_{1}{\bf q}_{1}s\beta^{2}(1-{\bf m}_{1}){\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\langle\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}\|{\bf x}^{(p_{`})}\|_{2}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}\|{\bf y}^{(p_{2})}\|_{2}\rangle_{\gamma_{1}^{(1)}}.

To find
T_{2,3}^{d}
, we first write

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-p\sum_{p_{3}=1}^{l}\frac{\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{p_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p-1}}{\left(\sum_{i_{3}=1}^{l}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p}\right)^{2}}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\frac{dZ_{p_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}}{du^{(4,2)}}

(69)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-p\sum_{p_{3}=1}^{l}\frac{\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{p_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p-1}}{\left(\sum_{i_{3}=1}^{l}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p}\right)^{2}}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\frac{{\bf m}_{1}}{Z_{p_{3}}^{1-{\bf m}_{1}}}\frac{dZ_{p_{3}}}{du^{(4,2)}}.

From
[
104
]
’s (62) we also have

A combination of (

) and (
70
) gives

\displaystyle\frac{d}{du^{(4,2)}}\left(\frac{1}{\left(\sum_{i_{3}=1}^{l}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p}\right)}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-p\sum_{p_{3}=1}^{l}\frac{\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{p_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p-1}}{\left(\sum_{i_{3}=1}^{l}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p}\right)^{2}}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\frac{{\bf m}_{1}}{Z_{p_{3}}^{1-{\bf m}_{1}}}

(71)

\displaystyle\times s\sum_{p_{1}=1}^{l}(C_{p_{3}}^{(p_{1})})^{s-1}\sum_{p_{2}=1}^{l}\beta_{p_{1}}A_{p_{3}}^{(p_{1},p_{2})}\|{\bf y}^{(p_{2})}\|_{2}\sqrt{t}.

\displaystyle\times p\sum_{p_{3}=1}^{l}\frac{\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{p_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p-1}}{\left(\sum_{i_{3}=1}^{l}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p}\right)^{2}}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\frac{{\bf m}_{1}}{Z_{p_{3}}^{1-{\bf m}_{1}}}s\sum_{p_{1}=1}^{l}(C_{p_{3}}^{(p_{1})})^{s-1}\sum_{p_{2}=1}^{l}\beta_{p_{1}}A_{p_{3}}^{(p_{1},p_{2})}\|{\bf y}^{(p_{2})}\|_{2}\sqrt{t}\Bigg.\Bigg)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-s\sqrt{t}{\bf p}_{1}{\bf q}_{1}{\bf m}_{1}p{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\Bigg(\Bigg.\sum_{p_{3}=1}^{l}\frac{\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{p_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p}}{\left(\sum_{i_{3}=1}^{l}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p}\right)}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\frac{Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}}{{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}}\frac{(C^{(i_{1})})^{s}}{Z}\frac{A^{(i_{1},i_{2})}}{(C^{(i_{1})})}

\displaystyle\times\sum_{p_{3}=1}^{l}\frac{\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{p_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p}}{\left(\sum_{i_{3}=1}^{l}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p}\right)}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\frac{Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}}{{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}}\sum_{p_{1}=1}^{l}\frac{(C^{(p_{1})})^{s}}{Z}\sum_{p_{2}=1}^{l}\frac{A^{(p_{1},p_{2})}}{(C^{(p_{1})})}\beta_{p_{1}}\|{\bf y}^{(p_{2})}\|_{2}\Bigg.\Bigg).

We then note

2.1.3

–group – first level

We first recall on

\displaystyle+\sum_{p_{1}=1}^{l}{\mathbb{E}}_{G}\Bigg.\Bigg(\frac{\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p-1}(C_{i_{3}}^{(i_{1})})^{s-1}A_{i_{3}}^{(i_{1},i_{2})}{\bf y}_{j}^{(i_{2})}}{Z_{i_{3}}^{1-{\bf m}_{1}}}

We first set

\displaystyle\Theta_{G,1}

\displaystyle=

]

(87)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-s{\bf m}_{1}p\frac{(C_{i_{3}}^{(i_{1})})^{s-1}A_{i_{3}}^{(i_{1},i_{2})}{\bf y}_{j}^{(i_{2})}}{Z_{i_{3}}^{1-{\bf m}_{1}}}\sum_{p_{1}=1}^{l}\frac{({\bf x}^{(p_{1})})^{T}{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}}{\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}\|{\bf x}^{(p_{1})}\|_{2}}\sum_{p_{3}=1}^{l}\frac{\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{p_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p-1}}{\left(\sum_{i_{3}=1}^{l}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p}\right)^{2}}

\displaystyle\times{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{p_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}-1}(C_{p_{3}}^{(p_{1})})^{s-1}\sum_{p_{2}=1}^{l}A_{p_{3}}^{(p_{1},p_{2})}\beta_{p_{1}}{\bf y}_{j}^{(p_{2})}\sqrt{t}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-s{\bf m}_{1}p\frac{Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}}{\left(\sum_{i_{3}=1}^{l}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p}\right)}\frac{(C_{i_{3}}^{(i_{1})})^{s}A_{i_{3}}^{(i_{1},i_{2})}}{Z_{i_{3}}C_{i_{3}}^{(i_{1})}}\sum_{p_{3}=1}^{l}\frac{\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{p_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p}}{\left(\sum_{p_{3}=1}^{l}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{p_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p}\right)}

\displaystyle\times{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\frac{Z_{p_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}}{{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{p_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}}\sum_{p_{1}=1}^{l}\frac{(C_{p_{3}}^{(p_{1})})^{s}}{Z_{p_{3}}}\sum_{p_{2}=1}^{l}\frac{A_{p_{3}}^{(p_{1},p_{2})}}{C_{p_{3}}^{(p_{1})}}\beta_{p_{1}}{\bf y}_{j}^{(p_{2})}{\bf y}_{j}^{(i_{2})}\frac{({\bf x}^{(p_{1})})^{T}{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}}{\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}\|{\bf x}^{(p_{1})}\|_{2}}\sqrt{t}.

From (
2.1.3
) and (
87
), we then find

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}=1}^{l}\sum_{i_{2}=1}^{l}\sum_{j=1}^{m}\beta_{i_{1}}\frac{T_{G,j}^{d}}{\sqrt{t}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-s\beta^{2}{\bf m}_{1}p{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\langle({\bf x}^{(p_{1})})^{T}{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}({\bf y}^{(p_{2})})^{T}{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\rangle_{\gamma_{21}^{(1)}}.

(88)

To determine
T_{G,j}^{e}
, we first observe

\displaystyle\frac{d}{d{\bf u}_{j}^{(p_{1},1)}}\left(\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p-1}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(p-1)\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p-2}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\frac{dZ_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}}{d{\bf u}_{j}^{(p_{1},1)}}

(89)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\bf m}_{1}(p-1)\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p-2}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}-1}\frac{dZ_{i_{3}}}{d{\bf u}_{j}^{(p_{1},1)}},

and then utilize again
[
104
]
’s (73) to obtain

\frac{d}{d{\bf u}_{j}^{(p_{1},1)}}\left(\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p-1}\right)={\bf m}_{1}(p-1)\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p-2}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}-1}s(C_{i_{3}}^{(p_{1})})^{s-1}\sum_{p_{2}=1}^{l}A_{i_{3}}^{(p_{1},p_{2})}\beta_{p_{1}}{\bf y}_{j}^{(p_{2})}\sqrt{t}.

(90)

Plugging (

) into the expression for
\Theta_{G,3}
from (

) gives

\displaystyle\Theta_{G,3}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\Bigg.\Bigg(\frac{(C_{i_{3}}^{(i_{1})})^{s-1}A_{i_{3}}^{(i_{1},i_{2})}{\bf y}_{j}^{(i_{2})}}{\left(\sum_{i_{3}=1}^{l}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p}\right)Z_{i_{3}}^{1-{\bf m}_{1}}}\sum_{p_{1}=1}^{l}{\mathbb{E}}({\bf u}_{j}^{(i_{1},1)}{\bf u}_{j}^{(p_{1},1)})\frac{d}{d{\bf u}_{j}^{(p_{1},1)}}\Bigg.\Bigg(\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p-1}\Bigg.\Bigg)\Bigg.\Bigg)

(91)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle s{\bf m}_{1}(p-1)\frac{(C_{i_{3}}^{(i_{1})})^{s-1}A_{i_{3}}^{(i_{1},i_{2})}{\bf y}_{j}^{(i_{2})}}{\left(\sum_{i_{3}=1}^{l}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p}\right)Z_{i_{3}}^{1-{\bf m}_{1}}}\sum_{p_{1}=1}^{l}\frac{({\bf x}^{(p_{1})})^{T}{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}}{\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}\|{\bf x}^{(p_{1})}\|_{2}}

\displaystyle\times\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p-2}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}-1}(C_{i_{3}}^{(p_{1})})^{s-1}\sum_{p_{2}=1}^{l}A_{i_{3}}^{(p_{1},p_{2})}\beta_{p_{1}}{\bf y}_{j}^{(p_{2})}\sqrt{t}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle s{\bf m}_{1}(p-1)\sum_{i_{3}=1}^{l}\frac{\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p}}{\left(\sum_{p_{3}=1}^{l}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{p_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p}\right)}\frac{Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}}{{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}}\frac{(C_{i_{3}}^{(i_{1})})^{s}A_{i_{3}}^{(i_{1},i_{2})}}{Z_{i_{3}}C_{i_{3}}^{(i_{1})}}

\displaystyle\times{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\frac{Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}}{{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}}\sum_{p_{1}=1}^{l}\frac{(C_{i_{3}}^{(p_{1})})^{s}}{Z_{i_{3}}}\sum_{p_{2}=1}^{l}\frac{A_{i_{3}}^{(p_{1},p_{2})}}{C_{i_{3}}^{(p_{1})}}\beta_{p_{1}}{\bf y}_{j}^{(p_{2})}{\bf y}_{j}^{(i_{2})}\frac{({\bf x}^{(p_{1})})^{T}{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}}{\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}\|{\bf x}^{(p_{1})}\|_{2}}\sqrt{t}.

From (

) and (

), we then find

From (

(96)

\displaystyle-s\beta^{2}(1-{\bf m}_{1}){\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\langle({\bf x}^{(p_{1})})^{T}{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}({\bf y}^{(p_{2})})^{T}{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\rangle_{\gamma_{1}^{(1)}}

\displaystyle-{\bf p}_{1}s\beta^{2}(1-{\bf m}_{1}){\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\langle\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}\|{\bf x}^{(p_{1})}\|_{2}({\bf y}^{(p_{2})})^{T}{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\rangle_{\gamma_{1}^{(1)}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-s{\bf m}_{1}p{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\langle({\bf p}_{1}\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}\|{\bf x}^{(p_{1})}\|_{2}-({\bf x}^{(p_{1})})^{T}{\bf x}^{(i_{1})})({\bf q}_{1}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}\|{\bf y}^{(p_{2})}\|_{2}-({\bf y}^{(p_{2})})^{T}{\bf y}^{(i_{2})})\rangle_{\gamma_{21}^{(1)}}

\displaystyle\phi_{22}^{(1)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle s{\bf m}_{1}(p-1){\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\langle({\bf p}_{1}\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}\|{\bf x}^{(p_{1})}\|_{2}-({\bf x}^{(p_{1})})^{T}{\bf x}^{(i_{1})})({\bf q}_{1}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}\|{\bf y}^{(p_{2})}\|_{2}-({\bf y}^{(p_{2})})^{T}{\bf y}^{(i_{2})})\rangle_{\gamma_{22}^{(1)}}

\displaystyle=
