(101)

The following proposition summarizes the above results.

Proposition 1
.

Consider vector
{\bf p}=[{\bf p}_{0},{\bf p}_{1},{\bf p}_{2}]
with
{\bf p}_{0}\geq{\bf p}_{1}\geq{\bf p}_{2}=0
, vector
{\bf q}=[{\bf q}_{0},{\bf q}_{1},{\bf q}_{2}]
with
{\bf q}_{0}\geq{\bf q}_{1}\geq{\bf q}_{2}=0
, and a scalar
{\bf m}_{1}
. For
k\in\{1,2\}
let
G\in{\mathbb{R}}^{m\times n},u^{(4,k)}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{1},{\bf u}^{(2,k)}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{m\times 1}
, and
{\bf h}^{(k)}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{n\times 1}
be independent Gaussian objects with i.i.d. zero-mean components. Let the variances of the elements of
G
,
u^{(4,1)}
,
u^{(4,2)}
,
{\bf u}^{(2,1)}
,
{\bf u}^{(2,2)}
,
{\bf h}^{(1)}
, and
{\bf h}^{(2)}
be
1
,
{\bf p}_{0}{\bf q}_{0}-{\bf p}_{1}{\bf q}_{1}
,
{\bf p}_{1}{\bf q}_{1}
,
{\bf p}_{0}-{\bf p}_{1}
,
{\bf p}_{1}
,
{\bf q}_{0}-{\bf q}_{1}
, and
{\bf q}_{1}
, respectively. Let
{\mathcal{U}}_{k}=[u^{(4,k)},{\bf u}^{(2,k)},{\bf h}^{(k)}]
and assume that sets
{\mathcal{X}}=\{{\bf x}^{(1)},{\bf x}^{(2)},\dots,{\bf x}^{(l)}\}
,
\bar{{\mathcal{X}}}=\{\bar{{\bf x}}^{(1)},\bar{{\bf x}}^{(2)},\dots,\bar{{\bf x}}^{(l)}\}
, and
{\mathcal{Y}}=\{{\bf y}^{(1)},{\bf y}^{(2)},\dots,{\bf y}^{(l)}\}
where
{\bf x}^{(i)},\bar{{\bf x}}^{(i)}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{n}
and
{\bf y}^{(i)}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{m}
,
1\leq i\leq l
are given. Let
p,\beta\geq 0
and
s
be real numbers and let
f_{\bar{{\bf x}}^{(i)}}(\cdot):{\mathbb{R}}^{n}\rightarrow{\mathbb{R}}
be a given function. Consider

\psi(t)={\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\frac{1}{p|s|\sqrt{n}{\bf m}_{1}}\log\left(\sum_{i_{3}=1}^{l}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left(\sum_{i_{1}=1}^{l}\left(\sum_{i_{2}=1}^{l}e^{\beta D_{0}^{(i_{1},i_{2},i_{3})}}\right)^{s}\right)^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p}\right),

(102)

where

\displaystyle D_{0}^{(i_{1},i_{2},i_{3})}

\displaystyle\triangleq

\displaystyle\sqrt{t}({\bf y}^{(i_{2})})^{T}G{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}+\sqrt{1-t}\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}({\bf y}^{(i_{2})})^{T}({\bf u}^{(2,1)}+{\bf u}^{(2,2)})

\displaystyle+\sqrt{t}\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}(u^{(4,1)}+u^{(4,2)})+\sqrt{1-t}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}({\bf h}^{(1)}+{\bf h}^{(2)})^{T}{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}+f_{\bar{{\bf x}}^{(i_{3})}}({\bf x}^{(i_{1})}).

Then

\displaystyle\frac{d\psi(t)}{dt}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{\mbox{sign}(s)\beta^{2}}{2\sqrt{n}}\left(\phi_{1}^{(1)}+\phi_{21}^{(1)}+\phi_{22}^{(1)}+\phi_{01}^{(1)}+\phi_{02}^{(1)}\right),

(104)

where
\phi
’s are as in (
2.1.4
).

Proof.

Follows immediately as a consequence of the above presentation.
∎

3
Moving from the first to the second level of full lifting

In this section, we discuss how the first level results presented above extend to the second level. All key concepts needed for this extension are already present in Section
2
. However, one has to be very careful as to how to use them. Also, since the move from the first to the second level is pretty much repeated successively later on as one moves to higher levels, we proceed very carefully and formalize all general steps that can then readily be recalled on and utilized on higher levels. As usual, to facilitate the exposition, we try to parallel the analytical methodology of Section
2
as closely as possible. On occasion and when circumstances allow, we avoid unnecessary repetitions and proceed at a bit faster pace.

We again consider vectors
{\bf p}
and
{\bf q}
but this time
{\bf p}=[{\bf p}_{0},{\bf p}_{1},{\bf p}_{2},{\bf p}_{3}]
with
{\bf p}_{0}\geq{\bf p}_{1}\geq{\bf p}_{2}\geq{\bf p}_{3}=0
and
{\bf q}=[{\bf q}_{0},{\bf q}_{1},{\bf q}_{2},{\bf q}_{3}]
with
{\bf q}_{0}\geq{\bf q}_{1}\geq{\bf q}_{2}\geq{\bf q}_{3}=0
. In addition to
G\in{\mathbb{R}}^{m\times n}
with i.i.d. standard normal elements we now have random variables
u^{(4,1)}\sim{\mathcal{N}}(0,{\bf p}_{0}{\bf q}_{0}-{\bf p}_{1}{\bf q}_{1})
,
u^{(4,2)}\sim{\mathcal{N}}(0,{\bf p}_{1}{\bf q}_{1}-{\bf p}_{2}{\bf q}_{2})
, and
u^{(4,3)}\sim{\mathcal{N}}(0,{\bf p}_{2}{\bf q}_{2})
that are independent of
G
and among themselves. We now have a vector
{\bf m}=[{\bf m}_{1},{\bf m}_{2}]
and the following interpolating function
\psi(\cdot)

\psi(t)={\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{3}}\frac{1}{p|s|\sqrt{n}{\bf m}_{2}}\log\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\left(\left(\sum_{i_{3}=1}^{l}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p}\right)^{\frac{{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)\right),

(105)

with

\displaystyle Z_{i_{3}}

\displaystyle\triangleq

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}=1}^{l}\left(\sum_{i_{2}=1}^{l}e^{\beta D_{0}^{(i_{1},i_{2},i_{3})}}\right)^{s}

\displaystyle D_{0}^{(i_{1},i_{2},i_{3})}

\displaystyle\triangleq

\displaystyle\sqrt{t}({\bf y}^{(i_{2})})^{T}G{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}+\sqrt{1-t}\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}({\bf y}^{(i_{2})})^{T}({\bf u}^{(2,1)}+{\bf u}^{(2,2)}+{\bf u}^{(2,3)})

(106)

\displaystyle+\sqrt{t}\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}(u^{(4,1)}+u^{(4,2)}+u^{(4,3)})+\sqrt{1-t}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}({\bf h}^{(1)}+{\bf h}^{(2)}+{\bf h}^{(3)})^{T}{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}

\displaystyle+f_{\bar{{\bf x}}^{(i_{3})}}({\bf x}^{(i_{1})}),

and
{\mathcal{U}}_{k}=[u^{(4,k)},{\bf u}^{(2,k)},{\bf h}^{(2k)}]
,
k\in\{1,2,3\}
. In (
105
)
and (
3
),
{\bf u}^{(2,1)}
,
{\bf u}^{(2,2)}
, and
{\bf u}^{(2,3)}
are
m
dimensional Gaussians with variances
{\bf p}_{0}-{\bf p}_{1}
,
{\bf p}_{1}-{\bf p}_{2}
, and ,
{\bf p}_{2}
, respectively. Analogously,
{\bf h}^{(1)}
,
{\bf h}^{(2)}
, and
{\bf h}^{(3)}
are
n
dimensional Gaussians with variances
{\bf q}_{0}-{\bf q}_{1}
,
{\bf q}_{1}-{\bf q}_{2}
, and
{\bf q}_{2}
, respectively. All random quantities are zero-mean and independent among themselves. Following the convention established in (
2
), we set

\displaystyle\bar{{\bf u}}^{(i_{1},1)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{G{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}}{\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}}

\displaystyle{\bf u}^{(i_{1},3,1)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{({\bf h}^{(1)})^{T}{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}}{\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}}

\displaystyle{\bf u}^{(i_{1},3,2)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{({\bf h}^{(2)})^{T}{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}}{\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}}

\displaystyle{\bf u}^{(i_{1},3,3)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{({\bf h}^{(3)})^{T}{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}}{\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}}.

(107)

and recall that

\displaystyle\bar{{\bf u}}_{j}^{(i_{1},1)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{G_{j,1:n}{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}}{\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}},1\leq j\leq m.

(108)

It is not that difficult to see that for any
i_{1}
the elements of
{\bf u}^{(i_{1},1)}
are i.i.d. standard normals and the elements of
{\bf u}^{(i_{1},3,1)}
,
{\bf u}^{(i_{1},3,2)}
, and
{\bf u}^{(i_{1},3,3)}
are zero-mean Gaussians with respective variances
{\bf q}_{0}-{\bf q}_{1}
,
{\bf q}_{1}-{\bf q}_{2}
, and
{\bf q}_{2}
. We then rewrite (
105
) as

\psi(t)={\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{3}}\frac{1}{p|s|\sqrt{n}{\bf m}_{2}}\log\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\left(\sum_{i_{3}=1}^{l}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left(\sum_{i_{1}=1}^{l}\left(\sum_{i_{2}=1}^{l}A_{i_{3}}^{(i_{1},i_{2})}\right)^{s}\right)^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p}\right)^{\frac{{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}}\right),

(109)

where
\beta_{i_{1}}=\beta\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}
and now

\displaystyle B^{(i_{1},i_{2})}

\displaystyle\triangleq

\displaystyle\sqrt{t}({\bf y}^{(i_{2})})^{T}\bar{{\bf u}}^{(i_{1},1)}+\sqrt{1-t}({\bf y}^{(i_{2})})^{T}({\bf u}^{(2,1)}+{\bf u}^{(2,2)}+{\bf u}^{(2,3)})

\displaystyle D^{(i_{1},i_{2},i_{3})}

\displaystyle\triangleq

\displaystyle B^{(i_{1},i_{2})}+\sqrt{t}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}(u^{(4,1)}+u^{(4,2)}+u^{(4,3)})+\sqrt{1-t}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}({\bf u}^{(i_{1},3,1)}+{\bf u}^{(i_{1},3,2)}+{\bf u}^{(i_{1},3,3)})

\displaystyle+f_{\bar{{\bf x}}^{(i_{3})}}({\bf x}^{(i_{1})})

\displaystyle A_{i_{3}}^{(i_{1},i_{2})}

\displaystyle\triangleq

\displaystyle e^{\beta_{i_{1}}D^{(i_{1},i_{2},i_{3})}}

\displaystyle C_{i_{3}}^{(i_{1})}

\displaystyle\triangleq

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{2}=1}^{l}A^{(i_{1},i_{2})}

\displaystyle Z_{i_{3}}

\displaystyle\triangleq

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}=1}^{l}\left(\sum_{i_{2}=1}^{l}A_{i_{3}}^{(i_{1},i_{2})}\right)^{s}=\sum_{i_{1}=1}^{l}(C_{i_{3}}^{(i_{1})})^{s}.

(110)

To study monotonicity of
\psi(t)
, we again start by considering its derivative

\displaystyle\frac{d\psi(t)}{dt}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{d}{dt}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{3}}\frac{1}{p|s|\sqrt{n}{\bf m}_{2}}\log\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\left(\left(\sum_{i_{3}=1}^{l}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p}\right)^{\frac{{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)\right)\right)

(111)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{3}}\frac{1}{p|s|\sqrt{n}{\bf m}_{2}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\left(\left(\sum_{i_{3}=1}^{l}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p}\right)^{\frac{{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)}\frac{d\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\left(\left(\sum_{i_{3}=1}^{l}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p}\right)^{\frac{{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)\right)}{dt}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{3},{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\sum_{i_{3}=1}^{l}\frac{\left(\sum_{i_{3}=1}^{l}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p}\right)^{\frac{{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}-1}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p-1}}{|s|\sqrt{n}{\bf m}_{1}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\left(\left(\sum_{i_{3}=1}^{l}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p}\right)^{\frac{{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)}\frac{d{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}}{dt}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{3},{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\sum_{i_{3}=1}^{l}\frac{\left(\sum_{i_{3}=1}^{l}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p}\right)^{\frac{{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}-1}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p-1}}{|s|\sqrt{n}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\left(\left(\sum_{i_{3}=1}^{l}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p}\right)^{\frac{{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\frac{1}{Z_{i_{3}}^{1-{\bf m}_{1}}}\frac{dZ_{i_{3}}}{dt}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{3},{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\sum_{i_{3}=1}^{l}\frac{\left(\sum_{i_{3}=1}^{l}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p}\right)^{\frac{{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}-1}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p-1}}{|s|\sqrt{n}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\left(\left(\sum_{i_{3}=1}^{l}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p}\right)^{\frac{{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\frac{1}{Z_{i_{3}}^{1-{\bf m}_{1}}}\frac{d\left(\sum_{i_{1}=1}^{l}\left(\sum_{i_{2}=1}^{l}A_{i_{3}}^{(i_{1},i_{2})}\right)^{s}\right)}{dt}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{3},{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\sum_{i_{3}=1}^{l}\frac{\left(\sum_{i_{3}=1}^{l}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p}\right)^{\frac{{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}-1}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p-1}}{|s|\sqrt{n}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\left(\left(\sum_{i_{3}=1}^{l}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p}\right)^{\frac{{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)}s{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\frac{1}{Z_{i_{3}}^{1-{\bf m}_{1}}}\sum_{i=1}^{l}(C_{i_{3}}^{(i_{1})})^{s-1}

\displaystyle\times\sum_{i_{2}=1}^{l}\beta_{i_{1}}A_{i_{3}}^{(i_{1},i_{2})}\frac{dD^{(i_{1},i_{2},i_{3})}}{dt},

where

\frac{dD^{(i_{1},i_{2},i_{3})}}{dt}=\left(\frac{dB^{(i_{1},i_{2})}}{dt}+\frac{\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}(u^{(4,1)}+u^{(4,2)}+u^{(4,3)})}{2\sqrt{t}}-\frac{\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}({\bf u}^{(i_{1},3,1)}+{\bf u}^{(i_{1},3,2)}+{\bf u}^{(i_{1},3,3)})}{2\sqrt{1-t}}\right).

(112)

From (
3
) we find

\displaystyle\frac{dB^{(i_{1},i_{2})}}{dt}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{d\left(\sqrt{t}({\bf y}^{(i_{2})})^{T}\bar{{\bf u}}^{(i_{1},1)}+\sqrt{1-t}({\bf y}^{(i_{2})})^{T}({\bf u}^{(2,1)}+{\bf u}^{(2,2)}+{\bf u}^{(2,3)})\right)}{dt}

(113)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{j=1}^{m}\left(\frac{{\bf y}_{j}^{(i_{2})}\bar{{\bf u}}_{j}^{(i_{1},1)}}{2\sqrt{t}}-\frac{{\bf y}_{j}^{(i_{2})}{\bf u}_{j}^{(2,1)}}{2\sqrt{1-t}}-\frac{{\bf y}_{j}^{(i_{2})}{\bf u}_{j}^{(2,2)}}{2\sqrt{1-t}}-\frac{{\bf y}_{j}^{(i_{2})}{\bf u}_{j}^{(2,3)}}{2\sqrt{1-t}}\right).

Analogously to (
17
) we then write

\frac{d\psi(t)}{dt}=\frac{\mbox{sign}(s)}{2\beta\sqrt{n}}\sum_{i_{1}=1}^{l}\sum_{i_{2}=1}^{l}\beta_{i_{1}}\left(T_{G}+T_{2}+T_{1}\right),

(114)

where

\displaystyle T_{G}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{j=1}^{m}\frac{T_{G,j}}{\sqrt{t}}

\displaystyle T_{3}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\sum_{j=1}^{m}\frac{T_{3,1,j}}{\sqrt{1-t}}-\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}\frac{T_{3,2}}{\sqrt{1-t}}+\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}\frac{T_{3,3}}{\sqrt{t}}

\displaystyle T_{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\sum_{j=1}^{m}\frac{T_{2,1,j}}{\sqrt{1-t}}-\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}\frac{T_{2,2}}{\sqrt{1-t}}+\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}\frac{T_{2,3}}{\sqrt{t}}

\displaystyle T_{1}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\sum_{j=1}^{m}\frac{T_{1,1,j}}{\sqrt{1-t}}-\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}\frac{T_{1,2}}{\sqrt{1-t}}+\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}\frac{T_{1,3}}{\sqrt{t}},

(115)

and for
k\in\{1,2,3\}

\displaystyle T_{G,j}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{3},{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\left(\sum_{i_{3}=1}^{l}\frac{\left(\sum_{i_{3}=1}^{l}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p}\right)^{\frac{{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}-1}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p-1}}{{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\left(\left(\sum_{i_{3}=1}^{l}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p}\right)^{\frac{{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\frac{(C_{i_{3}}^{(i_{1})})^{s-1}A_{i_{3}}^{(i_{1},i_{2})}{\bf y}_{j}^{(i_{2})}\bar{{\bf u}}_{j}^{(i_{1},1)}}{Z_{i_{3}}^{1-{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)

\displaystyle T_{k,1,j}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{3},{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\left(\sum_{i_{3}=1}^{l}\frac{\left(\sum_{i_{3}=1}^{l}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p}\right)^{\frac{{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}-1}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p-1}}{{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\left(\left(\sum_{i_{3}=1}^{l}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p}\right)^{\frac{{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\frac{(C_{i_{3}}^{(i_{1})})^{s-1}A_{i_{3}}^{(i_{1},i_{2})}{\bf y}_{j}^{(i_{2})}{\bf u}_{j}^{(2,k)}}{Z_{i_{3}}^{1-{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)

\displaystyle T_{k,2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{3},{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\left(\sum_{i_{3}=1}^{l}\frac{\left(\sum_{i_{3}=1}^{l}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p}\right)^{\frac{{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}-1}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p-1}}{{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\left(\left(\sum_{i_{3}=1}^{l}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p}\right)^{\frac{{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\frac{(C_{i_{3}}^{(i_{1})})^{s-1}A_{i_{3}}^{(i_{1},i_{2})}{\bf u}^{(i_{1},3,k)}}{Z_{i_{3}}^{1-{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)

\displaystyle T_{k,3}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{3},{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\left(\sum_{i_{3}=1}^{l}\frac{\left(\sum_{i_{3}=1}^{l}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p}\right)^{\frac{{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}-1}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p-1}}{{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\left(\left(\sum_{i_{3}=1}^{l}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p}\right)^{\frac{{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\frac{(C_{i_{3}}^{(i_{1})})^{s-1}A_{i_{3}}^{(i_{1},i_{2})}u^{(4,k)}}{Z_{i_{3}}^{1-{\bf m}_{1}}}\right).

(116)

Following the practice established in Section
2
, each of the above ten terms will be handled separately.

3.1
Computing
\frac{d\psi(t)}{dt}
– second level

The three terms indexed by
k
are grouped into three groups,
T_{k}
,
k\in\{1,2,3\}
. As earlier, we carefully select the order in which we handle each of these groups and start with
T_{1}
, then move to
T_{2}
, and then to
T_{3}
. After all three
T_{k}
groups are handled we switch to
T_{G,j}
.

3.1.1
T_{1}
–group — second level

Paralleling what was done in Section
2.1.1
, each of the three terms from
T_{1}
–group is handled separately.

Determining

T_{1,1,j}

As in (
20
), the Gaussian integration by parts gives

\displaystyle T_{1,1,j}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{3},{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\left(\sum_{i_{3}=1}^{l}\frac{\left(\sum_{i_{3}=1}^{l}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p}\right)^{\frac{{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}-1}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p-1}}{{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\left(\left(\sum_{i_{3}=1}^{l}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p}\right)^{\frac{{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\frac{(C_{i_{3}}^{(i_{1})})^{s-1}A_{i_{3}}^{(i_{1},i_{2})}{\bf y}_{j}^{(i_{2})}{\bf u}_{j}^{(2,1)}}{Z_{i_{3}}^{1-{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)

(117)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle({\bf p}_{0}-{\bf p}_{1}){\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{3},{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\Bigg(\Bigg.\sum_{i_{3}=1}^{l}\frac{\left(\sum_{i_{3}=1}^{l}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p}\right)^{\frac{{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}-1}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p-1}}{{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\left(\left(\sum_{i_{3}=1}^{l}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p}\right)^{\frac{{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)}

\displaystyle\times{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left(\frac{d}{d\bar{{\bf u}}_{j}^{(2,1)}}\left(\frac{(C_{i_{3}}^{(i_{1})})^{s-1}A_{i_{3}}^{(i_{1},i_{2})}{\bf y}_{j}^{(i_{2})}}{Z_{i_{3}}^{1-{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)\right)\Bigg.\Bigg).

Moreover, similarly to (
21
), we also have

\displaystyle T_{1,1,j}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle({\bf p}_{0}-{\bf p}_{1}){\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{3},{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\left(\sum_{i_{3}=1}^{l}\frac{\left(\sum_{i_{3}=1}^{l}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p}\right)^{\frac{{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}-1}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p-1}}{{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\left(\left(\sum_{i_{3}=1}^{l}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p}\right)^{\frac{{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)}\left(\Theta_{1}+\Theta_{2}\right)\right),

(118)

where
\Theta_{1}
and
\Theta_{2}
are as in (
2.1.1
). Analogously to (
2.1.1
), we write

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}=1}^{l}\sum_{i_{2}=1}^{l}\sum_{j=1}^{m}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\left(\sum_{i_{3}=1}^{l}\frac{\left(\sum_{i_{3}=1}^{l}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p}\right)^{\frac{{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}-1}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p-1}}{{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\left(\left(\sum_{i_{3}=1}^{l}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p}\right)^{\frac{{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)}\frac{\beta_{i_{1}}\Theta_{1}}{\sqrt{1-t}}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle L^{(2)}_{1}+L^{(2)}_{2},

(119)

where

\displaystyle L_{1}^{(2)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\left(\sum_{i_{3}=1}^{l}\frac{\left(\sum_{i_{3}=1}^{l}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p}\right)^{\frac{{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}-1}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p-1}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}}{{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\left(\left(\sum_{i_{3}=1}^{l}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p}\right)^{\frac{{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)}\sum_{i_{1}=1}^{l}\frac{(C_{i_{3}}^{(i_{1})})^{s}}{Z_{i_{3}}}\sum_{i_{2}=1}^{l}\frac{A_{i_{3}}^{(i_{1},i_{2})}}{C_{i_{3}}^{(i_{1})}}\beta_{i_{1}}^{2}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle L_{2}^{(2)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\Bigg(\Bigg.\sum_{i_{3}=1}^{l}\frac{\left(\sum_{i_{3}=1}^{l}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p}\right)^{\frac{{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}-1}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p-1}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}}{{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\left(\left(\sum_{i_{3}=1}^{l}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p}\right)^{\frac{{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)}

(120)

\displaystyle\times\frac{d}{d{\bf u}_{j}^{(2,2)}}\left(\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p-1}\right)\Bigg.\Bigg).

(135)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-({\bf p}_{1}-{\bf p}_{2})s\beta^{2}(1-{\bf m}_{1}){\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{3},{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\langle\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}\|{\bf x}^{(p_{1})}\|_{2}({\bf y}^{(p_{2})})^{T}{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\rangle_{\gamma_{1}^{(2)}}.

\displaystyle T_{2,1,j}^{d}

\displaystyle\times\Bigg.\Bigg(-p\sum_{p_{3}=1}^{l}\frac{\left(1-\frac{{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{p_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p-1}}{\left(\sum_{i_{3}=1}^{l}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p}\right)^{2-\frac{{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}}}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\frac{dZ_{p_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}}{d{\bf u}_{j}^{(2,2)}}\Bigg.\Bigg)\Bigg.\Bigg)

\displaystyle\times\sum_{i_{3}=1}^{l}\frac{\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p}}{\left(\sum_{i_{3}=1}^{l}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p}\right)}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\frac{Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}}{{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}}\frac{(C_{i_{3}}^{(i_{1})})^{s}}{Z_{i_{3}}}\frac{A_{i_{3}}^{(i_{1},i_{2})}}{C_{i_{3}}^{(i_{1})}}{\bf y}_{j}^{(i_{2})}

(138)

Switching the focus to

\displaystyle\frac{d}{d{\bf u}_{j}^{(2,2)}}\left(\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p-1}\right)=(p-1)\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p-2}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\frac{dZ_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}}{d{\bf u}_{j}^{(2,2)}}.

Relying again on (

\displaystyle T_{2,1,j}^{d}

\displaystyle=

(142)

Determining

Gaussian integration by parts gives

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\times{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\Bigg.\Bigg(\sum_{p_{1}=1}^{l}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}({\bf u}^{(i_{1},3,2)}{\bf u}^{(p_{1},3,2)})\frac{d}{d{\bf u}^{(p_{1},3,2)}}\Bigg.\Bigg(\frac{(C_{i_{3}}^{(i_{1})})^{s-1}A_{i_{3}}^{(i_{1},i_{2})}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p-1}}{Z^{1-{\bf m}_{1}}\left(\sum_{i_{3}=1}^{l}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p}\right)^{1-\frac{{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}}}\Bigg.\Bigg)\Bigg.\Bigg)\Bigg.\Bigg)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle T_{2,2}^{c}+T_{2,2}^{d}+T_{2,2}^{e},

where

\displaystyle T_{2,2}^{c}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{2},{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\Bigg(\Bigg.\sum_{i_{3}=1}^{l}\frac{\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p-1}}{{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\left(\left(\sum_{i_{3}=1}^{l}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p}\right)^{\frac{{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)}\frac{(C_{i_{3}}^{(i_{1})})^{s-1}A_{i_{3}}^{(i_{1},i_{2})}}{Z_{i_{3}}^{1-{\bf m}_{1}}}

\displaystyle\times\sum_{p_{1}=1}^{l}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}({\bf u}^{(i_{1},3,2)}{\bf u}^{(p_{1},3,2)})\frac{d}{d{\bf u}^{(p_{1},3,2)}}\Bigg(\Bigg.\left(\sum_{i_{3}=1}^{l}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p}\right)^{\frac{{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}-1}\Bigg)\Bigg.\Bigg)\Bigg.

\displaystyle T_{2,2}^{e}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{2},{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\Bigg(\Bigg.\sum_{i_{3}=1}^{l}\frac{\left(\sum_{i_{3}=1}^{l}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p}\right)^{\frac{{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}-1}}{{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\left(\left(\sum_{i_{3}=1}^{l}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p}\right)^{\frac{{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)}\frac{(C_{i_{3}}^{(i_{1})})^{s-1}A_{i_{3}}^{(i_{1},i_{2})}}{Z_{i_{3}}^{1-{\bf m}_{1}}}

(144)

\displaystyle\times\sum_{p_{1}=1}^{l}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}({\bf u}^{(i_{1},3,2)}{\bf u}^{(p_{1},3,2)})\frac{d}{d{\bf u}^{(p_{1},3,2)}}\left(\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p-1}\right)\Bigg.\Bigg).

Since

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}({\bf u}^{(i_{1},3,2)}{\bf u}^{(p_{1},3,2)})

we have that

scaled by

\displaystyle({\bf q}_{1}-{\bf q}_{2})\beta^{2}\Bigg(\Bigg.{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{3}}\langle\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}^{2}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}^{2}\rangle_{\gamma_{01}^{(2)}}

\displaystyle+(s-1){\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{3}}\langle\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}^{2}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}\|{\bf y}^{(p_{2})}\|_{2}\rangle_{\gamma_{02}^{(2)}}\Bigg.\Bigg)

\displaystyle-({\bf q}_{1}-{\bf q}_{2})s\beta^{2}(1-{\bf m}_{1}){\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{3}}\langle({\bf x}^{(p_{1})})^{T}{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}\|{\bf y}^{(p_{2})}\|_{2}\rangle_{\gamma_{1}^{(2)}}.

To determine
T_{2,2}^{d}
, we start with

\displaystyle\frac{d}{d{\bf u}^{(p_{1},3,2)}}\left(\left(\sum_{i_{3}=1}^{l}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p}\right)^{\frac{{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}-1}\right)=-p\sum_{p_{3}=1}^{l}\frac{\left(1-\frac{{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{p_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p-1}}{\left(\sum_{i_{3}=1}^{l}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p}\right)^{2-\frac{{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}}}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\frac{dZ_{p_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}}{d{\bf u}^{(p_{1},3,2)}}.

(147)

From (
56
) we also have

\displaystyle\frac{dZ_{p_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}}{d{\bf u}^{(p_{1},3,2)}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{{\bf m}_{1}}{Z_{p_{3}}^{1-{\bf m}_{1}}}s(C_{p_{3}}^{(p_{1})})^{s-1}\sum_{p_{2}=1}^{l}\beta_{p_{1}}A_{p_{3}}^{(p_{1},p_{2})}\|{\bf y}^{(p_{2})}\|_{2}\sqrt{1-t}.

A combination of (
147
) and (
148
) then gives

\displaystyle\frac{d}{d{\bf u}^{(p_{1},3,2)}}\left(\left(\sum_{i_{3}=1}^{l}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p}\right)^{\frac{{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}-1}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-p\sum_{p_{3}=1}^{l}\frac{\left(1-\frac{{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{p_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p-1}}{\left(\sum_{i_{3}=1}^{l}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p}\right)^{2-\frac{{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}}}

\displaystyle\times{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\frac{{\bf m}_{1}}{Z_{p_{3}}^{1-{\bf m}_{1}}}s(C_{p_{3}}^{(p_{1})})^{s-1}\sum_{p_{2}=1}^{l}\beta_{p_{1}}A_{p_{3}}^{(p_{1},p_{2})}\|{\bf y}^{(p_{2})}\|_{2}\sqrt{1-t}.

Combining further (
3.1.2
) and (
3.1.2
) we obtain the following analogue to (
58
)

\displaystyle T_{2,2}^{d}

, we start with

\displaystyle\frac{d}{d\bar{{\bf u}}_{j}^{(p_{1},1)}}\left(\frac{1}{{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\left(\left(\sum_{i_{3}=1}^{l}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p}\right)^{\frac{{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\frac{\frac{{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}\left(\left(\sum_{i_{3}=1}^{l}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p}\right)^{\frac{{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}-1}\right)}{\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\left(\left(\sum_{i_{3}=1}^{l}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p}\right)^{\frac{{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)\right)^{2}}

\displaystyle\times p\sum_{p_{3}=1}^{l}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{p_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p-1}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\frac{dZ_{p_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}}{d\bar{{\bf u}}_{j}^{(p_{1},1)}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\frac{\frac{{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}\left(\left(\sum_{i_{3}=1}^{l}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p}\right)^{\frac{{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}-1}\right)}{\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\left(\left(\sum_{i_{3}=1}^{l}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p}\right)^{\frac{{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)\right)^{2}}

\displaystyle\times p\sum_{p_{3}=1}^{l}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{p_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p-1}

\displaystyle\times{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}{\bf m}_{1}\frac{Z_{p_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}}{Z_{p_{3}}}s(C_{p_{3}}^{(p_{1})})^{s-1}\sum_{p_{2}=1}^{l}A_{p_{3}}^{(p_{1},p_{2})}\beta_{p_{1}}{\bf y}_{j}^{(p_{2})}\sqrt{t}.

Combining the expression for
\Theta_{G,3}^{(2)}
from (
3.1.4
) with (
3.1.4
), we obtain

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{2},{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\Bigg(\Bigg.\sum_{i_{3}=1}^{l}\left(\sum_{i_{3}=1}^{l}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p}\right)^{\frac{{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}-1}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p-1}

(210)

\displaystyle\times\frac{(C_{i_{3}}^{(i_{1})})^{s-1}A_{i_{3}}^{(i_{1},i_{2})}{\bf y}_{j}^{(i_{2})}}{Z_{i_{3}}^{1-{\bf m}_{1}}}\sum_{p_{1}=1}^{l}{\mathbb{E}}_{G}({\bf u}_{j}^{(i_{1},1)}{\bf u}_{j}^{(p_{1},1)}){\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\frac{\frac{{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}\left(\left(\sum_{i_{3}=1}^{l}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p}\right)^{\frac{{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}-1}\right)}{\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\left(\left(\sum_{i_{3}=1}^{l}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p}\right)^{\frac{{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)\right)^{2}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle s\beta^{2}{\bf m}_{2}p\Bigg(\Bigg.{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\frac{\left(\sum_{i_{3}=1}^{l}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p}\right)^{\frac{{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}}}{{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\left(\left(\sum_{i_{3}=1}^{l}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p}\right)^{\frac{{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)}\sum_{i_{3}=1}^{l}\frac{\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p}}{\sum_{i_{3}=1}^{l}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p}}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\frac{Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}}{{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}}

\displaystyle\times\frac{1}{Z_{p_{3}}}(C_{p_{3}}^{(p_{1})})^{s-1}\sum_{p_{2}=1}^{l}A_{p_{3}}^{(p_{1},p_{2})}{\bf y}_{j}^{(p_{2})}\sqrt{t}\Bigg.\Bigg).

\displaystyle-s\beta^{2}({\bf p}_{1}{\bf q}_{1}-{\bf p}_{2}{\bf q}_{2})({\bf m}_{1}-{\bf m}_{2})p{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{3}}\langle\|{\bf x}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}\|{\bf x}^{(p_{2})}\|_{2}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}\|{\bf y}^{(p_{2})}\rangle_{\gamma_{21}^{(2)}}

\displaystyle+s\beta^{2}({\bf p}_{1}{\bf q}_{1}-{\bf p}_{2}{\bf q}_{2}){\bf m}_{1}(p-1){\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{3}}\langle\|{\bf x}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}\|{\bf x}^{(p_{2})}\|_{2}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}\|{\bf y}^{(p_{2})}\rangle_{\gamma_{22}^{(2)}}

\displaystyle+{\bf p}_{2}{\bf q}_{2}\beta^{2}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{3}}\langle\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}^{2}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}^{2}\rangle_{\gamma_{01}^{(2)}}+(s-1){\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{3}}\langle\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}^{2}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}\|{\bf y}^{(p_{2})}\|_{2}\rangle_{\gamma_{02}^{(2)}}\right)

\displaystyle-{\bf p}_{2}{\bf q}_{2}s\beta^{2}(1-{\bf m}_{1}){\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{3}}\langle\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}\|{\bf x}^{(p_{`})}\|_{2}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}\|{\bf y}^{(p_{2})}\|_{2}\rangle_{\gamma_{1}^{(2)}}

\displaystyle-s\beta^{2}{\bf p}_{2}{\bf q}_{2}({\bf m}_{1}-{\bf m}_{2})p{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{3}}\langle\|{\bf x}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}\|{\bf x}^{(p_{2})}\|_{2}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}\|{\bf y}^{(p_{2})}\rangle_{\gamma_{21}^{(2)}}

\displaystyle+s\beta^{2}{\bf p}_{2}{\bf q}_{2}{\bf m}_{1}(p-1){\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{3}}\langle\|{\bf x}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}\|{\bf x}^{(p_{2})}\|_{2}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}\|{\bf y}^{(p_{2})}\rangle_{\gamma_{22}^{(2)}}

\displaystyle-s\beta^{2}{\bf p}_{2}{\bf q}_{2}{\bf m}_{2}p{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{3}}\langle\|{\bf x}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}\|{\bf x}^{(p_{2})}\|_{2}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}\|{\bf y}^{(p_{2})}\rangle_{\gamma_{3}^{(2)}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\bf p}_{0}{\bf q}_{0}\beta^{2}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{3}}\langle\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}^{2}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}^{2}\rangle_{\gamma_{01}^{(2)}}+(s-1){\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{3}}\langle\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}^{2}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}\|{\bf y}^{(p_{2})}\|_{2}\rangle_{\gamma_{02}^{(2)}}\right)

\displaystyle-{\bf p}_{0}{\bf q}_{0}s\beta^{2}(1-{\bf m}_{1}){\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{3}}\langle\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}\|{\bf x}^{(p_{`})}\|_{2}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}\|{\bf y}^{(p_{2})}\|_{2}\rangle_{\gamma_{1}^{(2)}}

\displaystyle-s\beta^{2}{\bf p}_{1}{\bf q}_{1}({\bf m}_{1}-{\bf m}_{2})p{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{3}}\langle\|{\bf x}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}\|{\bf x}^{(p_{2})}\|_{2}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}\|{\bf y}^{(p_{2})}\rangle_{\gamma_{21}^{(2)}}

\displaystyle+s\beta^{2}{\bf p}_{1}{\bf q}_{1}{\bf m}_{1}(p-1){\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{3}}\langle\|{\bf x}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}\|{\bf x}^{(p_{2})}\|_{2}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}\|{\bf y}^{(p_{2})}\rangle_{\gamma_{21}^{(2)}}

\displaystyle-s\beta^{2}{\bf p}_{2}{\bf q}_{2}{\bf m}_{2}p{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{3}}\langle\|{\bf x}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}\|{\bf x}^{(p_{2})}\|_{2}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}\|{\bf y}^{(p_{2})}\rangle_{\gamma_{3}^{(2)}}.

Finally, a combination of (
213
) and (
215
)-(
218
) gives

\displaystyle\frac{d\psi({\mathcal{X}},{\mathcal{Y}},{\bf q},{\bf m},\beta,s,t)}{dt}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{\mbox{sign}(s)\beta}{2\sqrt{n}}\left(\phi_{1}^{(2)}+\phi_{21}^{(2)}+\phi_{22}^{(2)}+\phi_{3}^{(2)}+\phi_{01}^{(2)}+\phi_{02}^{(2)}\right),

(219)

where

\displaystyle\phi_{1}^{(2)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-s(1-{\bf m}_{1}){\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{3}}\langle({\bf p}_{0}\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}\|{\bf x}^{(p_{1})}\|_{2}-({\bf x}^{(p_{1})})^{T}{\bf x}^{(i_{1})})({\bf q}_{0}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}\|{\bf y}^{(p_{2})}\|_{2}-({\bf y}^{(p_{2})})^{T}{\bf y}^{(i_{2})})\rangle_{\gamma_{1}^{(2)}}

\displaystyle\phi_{21}^{(2)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-s({\bf m}_{1}-{\bf m}_{2})p{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{3}}\langle({\bf p}_{1}\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}\|{\bf x}^{(p_{1})}\|_{2}-({\bf x}^{(p_{1})})^{T}{\bf x}^{(i_{1})})({\bf q}_{1}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}\|{\bf y}^{(p_{2})}\|_{2}-({\bf y}^{(p_{2})})^{T}{\bf y}^{(i_{2})})\rangle_{\gamma_{21}^{(2)}}

\displaystyle\phi_{22}^{(2)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle s{\bf m}_{1}(p-1){\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{3}}\langle({\bf p}_{1}\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}\|{\bf x}^{(p_{1})}\|_{2}-({\bf x}^{(p_{1})})^{T}{\bf x}^{(i_{1})})({\bf q}_{1}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}\|{\bf y}^{(p_{2})}\|_{2}-({\bf y}^{(p_{2})})^{T}{\bf y}^{(i_{2})})\rangle_{\gamma_{22}^{(2)}}

\displaystyle\phi_{3}^{(2)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-s{\bf m}_{2}p{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{3}}\langle({\bf p}_{2}\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}\|{\bf x}^{(p_{1})}\|_{2}-({\bf x}^{(p_{1})})^{T}{\bf x}^{(i_{1})})({\bf q}_{2}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}\|{\bf y}^{(p_{2})}\|_{2}-({\bf y}^{(p_{2})})^{T}{\bf y}^{(i_{2})})\rangle_{\gamma_{3}^{(2)}}

\displaystyle\phi_{01}^{(2)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(1-{\bf p}_{0})(1-{\bf q}_{0}){\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{3}}\langle\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}^{2}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}^{2}\rangle_{\gamma_{01}^{(2)}}

\displaystyle\phi_{02}^{(2)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(1-{\bf p}_{0}){\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{3}}\left\langle\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}^{2}\left({\bf q}_{0}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}\|{\bf y}^{(p_{2})}\|_{2}-({\bf y}^{(p_{2})})^{T}{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\right)\right\rangle_{\gamma_{02}^{(2)}}.

(220)

The above results are summarized in the following proposition.

Proposition 2
.

For
k=\{1,2,3\}
let components of
G\in{\mathbb{R}}^{m\times n},u^{(4,k)}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{1},{\bf u}^{(2,k)}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{m\times 1}
, and
{\bf h}^{(k)}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{n\times 1}
be independent zero-mean Gaussians. For vectors
{\bf m}=[{\bf m}_{1},{\bf m}_{2}]
,
{\bf p}=[{\bf p}_{0},{\bf p}_{1},{\bf p}_{2},{\bf p}_{3}]
with
{\bf p}_{0}\geq{\bf p}_{1}\geq{\bf p}_{2}\geq{\bf p}_{3}=0
and
{\bf q}=[{\bf q}_{0},{\bf q}_{1},{\bf q}_{2},{\bf q}_{3}]
with
{\bf q}_{0}\geq{\bf q}_{1}\geq{\bf q}_{2}\geq{\bf q}_{3}=0
, let the variances of the elements of
G
,
u^{(4,k)}
,
{\bf u}^{(2,k)}
, and
{\bf h}^{(k)}
be
1
,
{\bf p}_{k-1}{\bf q}_{k-1}-{\bf p}_{k}{\bf q}_{k}
,
{\bf p}_{k-1}-{\bf p}_{k}
,
{\bf q}_{k-1}-{\bf q}_{k}
, respectively.
Let sets
{\mathcal{X}},\bar{{\mathcal{X}}},{\mathcal{Y}}
, scalars
\beta
,
p
,
s
, and function
f_{\bar{{\bf x}}^{(i_{3})}}(\cdot)
be as in Proposition

1
. Set
{\mathcal{U}}_{k}=[u^{(4,k)},{\bf u}^{(2,k)},{\bf h}^{(2k)}]

and consider the following

\psi(t)={\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{3}}\frac{1}{p|s|\sqrt{n}{\bf m}_{2}}\log\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\left(\left(\sum_{i_{3}=1}^{l}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p}\right)^{\frac{{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)\right),

(221)

where

\displaystyle Z_{i_{3}}

\displaystyle\triangleq

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}=1}^{l}\left(\sum_{i_{2}=1}^{l}e^{\beta D_{0}^{(i_{1},i_{2},i_{3})}}\right)^{s}

\displaystyle D_{0}^{(i_{1},i_{2},i_{3})}

\displaystyle\triangleq

\displaystyle\sqrt{t}({\bf y}^{(i_{2})})^{T}G{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}+\sqrt{1-t}\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}({\bf y}^{(i_{2})})^{T}({\bf u}^{(2,1)}+{\bf u}^{(2,2)}+{\bf u}^{(2,3)})

(222)

\displaystyle+\sqrt{t}\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}(u^{(4,1)}+u^{(4,2)}+u^{(4,3)})+\sqrt{1-t}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}({\bf h}^{(1)}+{\bf h}^{(2)}+{\bf h}^{(3)})^{T}{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}

\displaystyle+f_{\bar{{\bf x}}^{(i_{3})}}({\bf x}^{(i_{1})}).

Then

\displaystyle\frac{d\psi(t)}{dt}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{\mbox{sign}(s)\beta}{2\sqrt{n}}\left(\phi_{1}^{(2)}+\phi_{21}^{(2)}+\phi_{22}^{(2)}+\phi_{3}^{(2)}+\phi_{01}^{(2)}+\phi_{02}^{(2)}\right),

(223)

where
\phi
’s are as in (
3.1.5
) and
\gamma
’s are as defined in (
3.1.1
) and (
3.1.1
).

Proof.

Follows from the discussion presented above.
∎

4
Move to the
r
-th (
r\geq 3
) level of full lifting

After introducing key conceptual ingredients in Section
2
, we in Section
3
formalized them into a sequence of mathematical steps needed to proceed to second and higher levels of lifting. In this section we present the final results obtained via applying the procedure from Section
3
to any level of lifting
r\in{\mathbb{N}}
. The presentation of
[
104
]
’s Section 4
turns out to be of great help in that regard. In fact, as will soon be clear, many of the results obtained therein apply here either in an unaltered fashion or with fairly minimal adjustments.

As in
[
104
]
’s Section 4, we now take
r\geq 3
and consider vectors
{\bf m}=[{\bf m}_{1},{\bf m}_{2},...,{\bf m}_{r}]
,

{\bf p}=[{\bf p}_{0},{\bf p}_{1},...,{\bf p}_{r},{\bf p}_{r+1}]
with
{\bf p}_{0}\geq{\bf p}_{1}\geq{\bf p}_{2}\geq\dots\geq{\bf p}_{r}\geq{\bf p}_{r+1}=0
and
{\bf q}=[{\bf q}_{0},{\bf q}_{1},{\bf q}_{2},\dots,{\bf q}_{r},{\bf q}_{r+1}]
with
{\bf q}_{0}\geq{\bf q}_{1}\geq{\bf q}_{2}\geq\dots\geq{\bf q}_{r}\geq{\bf q}_{r+1}=0
. Also, we assume that the elements of
G\in{\mathbb{R}}^{m\times n}
and
u^{(4,k)}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{1},{\bf u}^{(2,k)}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{m\times 1}
, and
{\bf h}^{(k)}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{n\times 1}
(for any
k\in\{1,2,\dots,r+1\}
) are independent zero-mean normals. Moreover, he variances of the components of
G
,
u^{(4,k)}
,
{\bf u}^{(2,k)}
, and
{\bf h}^{(k)}
are assumed to be
1
,
{\bf p}_{k-1}{\bf q}_{k-1}-{\bf p}_{k}{\bf q}_{k}
,
{\bf p}_{k-1}-{\bf p}_{k}
,
{\bf q}_{k-1}-{\bf q}_{k}
, respectively. Similarly to what was done earlier, we also set
{\mathcal{U}}_{k}\triangleq[u^{(4,k)},{\bf u}^{(2,k)},{\bf h}^{(2k)}]
and assume that sets
{\mathcal{X}}
,
\bar{{\mathcal{X}}}
,
{\mathcal{Y}}
, scalars
\beta
,
p
,
s
, and function
f_{\bar{{\bf x}}^{(i_{3})}}(\cdot)
are as in Proposition
1
. We then focus on the following function

\psi(t)={\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{r+1}}\frac{1}{\beta|s|\sqrt{n}{\bf m}_{r}}\log\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{r}}\left(\dots\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\left(\left(\sum_{i_{3}=1}^{l}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p}\right)^{\frac{{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)\right)^{\frac{{\bf m}_{3}}{{\bf m}_{2}}}\dots\right)^{\frac{{\bf m}_{r}}{{\bf m}_{r-1}}}\right),

(224)

where

\displaystyle Z_{i_{3}}

\displaystyle\triangleq

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}=1}^{l}\left(\sum_{i_{2}=1}^{l}e^{\beta D_{0}^{(i_{1},i_{2},i_{3})}}\right)^{s}

\displaystyle D_{0}^{(i_{1},i_{2},i_{3})}

\displaystyle\triangleq

\displaystyle\sqrt{t}({\bf y}^{(i_{2})})^{T}G{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}+\sqrt{1-t}\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}({\bf y}^{(i_{2})})^{T}\left(\sum_{k=1}^{r+1}{\bf u}^{(2,k)}\right)

\displaystyle+\sqrt{t}\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}\left(\sum_{k=1}^{r+1}u^{(4,k)}\right)+\sqrt{1-t}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}\left(\sum_{k=1}^{r+1}{\bf h}^{(k)}\right)^{T}{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}+f_{\bar{{\bf x}}^{(i_{3})}}({\bf x}^{(i_{3})}).

Analogously to (
2
), we find it convenient to set

\displaystyle\bar{{\bf u}}^{(i_{1},1)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{G{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}}{\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}}

\displaystyle\bar{{\bf u}}^{(i_{1},3,k)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{({\bf h}^{(k)})^{T}{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}}{\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}},

(226)

and recall that

\displaystyle\bar{{\bf u}}_{j}^{(i_{1},1)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{G_{j,1:n}{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}}{\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}},1\leq j\leq m.

(227)

We observe that the elements of
\bar{{\bf u}}^{(i_{1},1)}
are i.i.d. standard normals, whereas the elements of
{\bf u}^{(2,k)}
are zero-mean independent Gaussians with variances
{\bf p}_{k-1}-{\bf p}_{k}
and the elements of
{\bf u}^{(i_{1},3,k)}
are zero-mean independent Gaussians with variances
{\bf q}_{k-1}-{\bf q}_{k}
. One can then rewrite (
224
) in the following way

\psi(t)={\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{r+1}}\frac{1}{p|s|\sqrt{n}{\bf m}_{r}}\log\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{r}}\left(\dots\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\left(\left(\sum_{i_{3}=1}^{l}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p}\right)^{\frac{{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)\right)^{\frac{{\bf m}_{3}}{{\bf m}_{2}}}\dots\right)^{\frac{{\bf m}_{r}}{{\bf m}_{r-1}}}\right),

(228)

where
\beta_{i_{1}}=\beta\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}
and now

\displaystyle B^{(i_{1},i_{2})}

\displaystyle\triangleq

\displaystyle\sqrt{t}({\bf y}^{(i_{2})})^{T}\bar{{\bf u}}^{(i_{1},1)}+\sqrt{1-t}({\bf y}^{(i_{2})})^{T}\left(\sum_{k=1}^{r+1}{\bf u}^{(2,k)}\right)

\displaystyle D^{(i_{1},i_{2},i_{3})}

\displaystyle\triangleq

\displaystyle B^{(i_{1},i_{2})}+\sqrt{t}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}\left(\sum_{k=1}^{r+1}u^{(4,k)}\right)+\sqrt{1-t}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}\left(\sum_{k=1}^{r+1}{\bf u}^{(i_{1},3,k)}\right)+f_{\bar{{\bf x}}^{(i_{3})}}({\bf x}^{(i_{1})})

\displaystyle A_{i_{3}}^{(i_{1},i_{2})}

\displaystyle\triangleq

\displaystyle e^{\beta_{i_{1}}D^{(i_{1},i_{2},i_{3})}}

\displaystyle C_{i_{3}}^{(i_{1})}

\displaystyle\triangleq

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{2}=1}^{l}A_{i_{3}}^{(i_{1},i_{2})}

\displaystyle Z_{i_{3}}

\displaystyle\triangleq

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}=1}^{l}\left(\sum_{i_{2}=1}^{l}A_{i_{3}}^{(i_{1},i_{2})}\right)^{s}=\sum_{i_{1}=1}^{l}(C_{i_{3}}^{(i_{1})})^{s}.

(229)

We set
{\bf m}_{0}=1
and analogously to
[
104
]
’s (199)

\displaystyle\zeta_{r}\triangleq{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{r}}\left(\dots\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\left(\left(\sum_{i_{3}=1}^{l}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{\frac{{\bf m}_{1}}{{\bf m}_{0}}}\right)^{p}\right)^{\frac{{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)\right)^{\frac{{\bf m}_{3}}{{\bf m}_{2}}}\dots\right)^{\frac{{\bf m}_{r}}{{\bf m}_{r-1}}},r\geq 2.

(230)

Then one has

\displaystyle\zeta_{k}={\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{k}}\left(\zeta_{k-1}\right)^{\frac{{\bf m}_{k}}{{\bf m}_{k-1}}},k\geq 2,\quad\mbox{and}\quad\zeta_{1}=\sum_{i_{3}=1}^{l}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{\frac{{\bf m}_{1}}{{\bf m}_{0}}}\right)^{p}.

(231)

Utilizing
[
104
]
’s (201) we write

\displaystyle\frac{d\psi(t)}{dt}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{r+1}}\frac{1}{p|s|\sqrt{n}{\bf m}_{1}\zeta_{r}}\prod_{k=r}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{k}}\zeta_{k-1}^{\frac{{\bf m}_{k}}{{\bf m}_{k-1}}-1}\frac{d\zeta_{1}}{dt}

(232)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{r+1}}\frac{1}{|s|\sqrt{n}{\bf m}_{1}\zeta_{r}}\prod_{k=r}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{k}}\zeta_{k-1}^{\frac{{\bf m}_{k}}{{\bf m}_{k-1}}-1}\sum_{i_{3}=1}^{l}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p-1}\frac{d{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}}{dt}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{r+1}}\frac{1}{|s|\sqrt{n}\zeta_{r}}\prod_{k=r}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{k}}\zeta_{k-1}^{\frac{{\bf m}_{k}}{{\bf m}_{k-1}}-1}\sum_{i_{3}=1}^{l}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p-1}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\frac{1}{Z_{i_{3}}^{1-{\bf m}_{1}}}\sum_{i=1}^{l}(C_{i_{3}}^{(i_{1})})^{s-1}

\displaystyle\times\sum_{i_{2}=1}^{l}\beta_{i_{1}}A_{i_{3}}^{(i_{1},i_{2})}\frac{dD^{(i_{1},i_{2},i_{3})}}{dt},

with product running in an
index decreasing order
and

\displaystyle\frac{dD^{(i_{1},i_{2},i_{3})}}{dt}=\left(\frac{dB^{(i_{1},i_{2})}}{dt}+\frac{\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}(\sum_{k=1}^{r+1}u^{(4,k)})}{2\sqrt{t}}-\frac{\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}(\sum_{k=1}^{r+1}{\bf u}^{(i_{1},3,k)})}{2\sqrt{1-t}}\right).

(233)

As shown in
[
104
]
’s (203), we have

\displaystyle\frac{dB^{(i_{1},i_{2})}}{dt}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{j=1}^{m}\left(\frac{{\bf y}_{j}^{(i_{2})}{\bf u}_{j}^{(i_{1},1)}}{2\sqrt{t}}-\frac{{\bf y}_{j}^{(i_{2})}\sum_{k=1}^{r+1}{\bf u}_{j}^{(2,k)}}{2\sqrt{1-t}}\right).

(234)

and then analogously to (
17
) (and
[
104
]
’s (203)-(205))

\frac{d\psi(t)}{dt}=\frac{\mbox{sign}(s)}{2\beta\sqrt{n}}\sum_{i_{1}=1}^{l}\sum_{i_{2}=1}^{l}\beta_{i_{1}}\left(T_{G}+\sum_{k=1}^{r+1}T_{k}\right),

(235)

where

\displaystyle T_{G}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{j=1}^{m}\frac{T_{G,j}}{\sqrt{t}}

\displaystyle T_{k}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\sum_{j=1}^{m}\frac{T_{k,1,j}}{\sqrt{1-t}}-\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}\frac{T_{k,2}}{\sqrt{1-t}}+\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}\frac{T_{k,3}}{\sqrt{t}},k\in\{1,2,\dots,r+1\}.

(236)

\displaystyle T_{G,j}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{r+1}}\left(\zeta_{r}^{-1}\prod_{v=r}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{v}}\zeta_{v-1}^{\frac{{\bf m}_{v}}{{\bf m}_{v-1}}-1}\sum_{i_{3}=1}^{l}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p-1}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\frac{(C_{i_{3}}^{(i_{1})})^{s-1}A_{i_{3}}^{(i_{1},i_{2})}{\bf y}_{j}^{(i_{2})}{\bf u}_{j}^{(i_{1},1)}}{Z_{i_{3}}^{1-{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)

\displaystyle T_{k,1,j}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{r+1}}\left(\zeta_{r}^{-1}\prod_{v=r}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{v}}\zeta_{v-1}^{\frac{{\bf m}_{v}}{{\bf m}_{v-1}}-1}\sum_{i_{3}=1}^{l}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p-1}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\frac{(C_{i_{3}}^{(i_{1})})^{s-1}A_{i_{3}}^{(i_{1},i_{2})}{\bf y}_{j}^{(i_{2})}{\bf u}_{j}^{(2,k)}}{Z_{i_{3}}^{1-{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)

\displaystyle T_{k,2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{r+1}}\left(\zeta_{r}^{-1}\prod_{v=r}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{v}}\zeta_{v-1}^{\frac{{\bf m}_{v}}{{\bf m}_{v-1}}-1}\sum_{i_{3}=1}^{l}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p-1}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\frac{(C_{i_{3}}^{(i_{1})})^{s-1}A_{i_{3}}^{(i_{1},i_{2})}{\bf u}^{(i_{1},3,k)}}{Z_{i_{3}}^{1-{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)

\displaystyle T_{k,3}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{r+1}}\left(\zeta_{r}^{-1}\prod_{v=r}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{v}}\zeta_{v-1}^{\frac{{\bf m}_{v}}{{\bf m}_{v-1}}-1}\sum_{i_{3}=1}^{l}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p-1}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\frac{(C_{i_{3}}^{(i_{1})})^{s-1}A_{i_{3}}^{(i_{1},i_{2})}u^{(4,k)}}{Z_{i_{3}}^{1-{\bf m}_{1}}}\right).

(237)

We first discuss the three sequences
\left(T_{k,1,j}\right)_{k=1:r+1}
,
\left(T_{k,2}\right)_{k=1:r+1}
, and
\left(T_{k,3}\right)_{k=1:r+1}
and then
T_{G,j}
. As Section
3
showed, the internal relations among the components in any of these sequences are for all that we need identical. It will therefore be sufficient to take one of them, say
\left(T_{k,1,j}\right)_{k=1:r+1}
, and show how the results of Section
3
immediately extend from
r=2
to any integer
r>2
. For the remaining two,
\left(T_{k,2}\right)_{k=1:r+1}
, and
\left(T_{k,3}\right)_{k=1:r+1}
, we avoid unnecessarily repeating the same strategy and instead quickly deduce the final results.

4.1
Successive scaling and canceling out

Following the strategy of
[
104
]
, we consider sequence
\left(T_{k,1,j}\right)_{k=1:r+1}
, take one component, say
T_{k_{1},1,j}
,
k_{1}\geq 2
, and show how it relates to the very next one,
T_{k_{1}+1,1,j}
. Two aspects of such a recursive relation play a key role: 1)
successive scaling
; and 2) appearance of an appropriately
reweightedly averaged (over a
\gamma
measure) new term
.

Utilizing
[
104
]
’s (207)-(209), we have

\displaystyle T_{k_{1},1,j}\hskip-4.33601pt

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\hskip-4.33601pt({\bf p}_{k_{1}-1}-{\bf p}_{k_{1}}){\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{r+1}}\Bigg(\Bigg.\zeta_{r}^{-1}\prod_{v=r}^{k_{1}+1}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{v}}\zeta_{v-1}^{\frac{{\bf m}_{v}}{{\bf m}_{v-1}}-1}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{k_{1}}}

\displaystyle\times\frac{(C_{i_{3}}^{(i_{1})})^{s-1}A_{i_{3}}^{(i_{1},i_{2})}{\bf y}_{j}^{(i_{2})}}{Z_{i_{3}}^{1-{\bf m}_{1}}}\frac{d}{d\bar{{\bf u}}_{j}^{(p_{1},1)}}\left(\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z_{i_{3}}^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{p-1}\right)\Bigg.\Bigg)

\displaystyle T_{G,j}^{d_{k_{1}-1}}

\displaystyle=
